# Round 6: candle ingestion, as implemented vs. the pending local-candle direction

## FINDINGS

### Ownership (questions 1–5, 9, 11): unchanged, rechecked
- **OFF mode** is the checked-in default (`application.yml:421`). The trader owns WebSocket and REST kline ingestion and writes `crypto_ai.candle`.
- **LIVE mode** is the only value that turns off direct ingestion (`BinanceWebSocketManager.java:28,97,127`; `MarketDataService.java:19,32`; `MarketDataBootstrapService.java:31,50,73`).
- **OBSERVE mode** therefore runs direct ingestion and the collector at the same time. In OBSERVE, the startup import also runs analysis and `processSignal` with origin `STARTUP` (`MarketDataBootstrapService.java:107-136`).
- **Production:** I did not access it and have no evidence about it.

### Correction 1: my round-5 race has more triggers than I said
The race itself holds: REST import closes an open `RUNTIME` row with no token, so the WebSocket's final message never creates a dispatchable transition.
- Mechanism: MySQL evaluates `ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE` left to right, and `closed=` is assigned last (`CandleRepository.java:188,203`). So the token test `IF(closed=0 AND …)` at `:201` sees the row as already closed. The read-back at `:120` then fails, and nothing is dispatched.
- I limited it to adding a coin. It is wider:
  - **Re-enabling a coin:** `PUT /api/administration/coins/{id}/enabled` also calls `activate` (`CoinConfigurationController.java:41`).
  - **A failed reload:** if `reload()` times out after 30 seconds, the exception is caught (`DynamicCoinActivationService.java:23`) and the import still runs (`:28`).
  - **The import endpoint (broadest):** `POST /api/market-data/import` (`MarketDataController.java:16-22`) can be called at runtime for any symbol that is already streaming. No reload is needed. I did not check whether it requires authentication.

### New 1: ordinary import skips the close grace period that reconciliation uses
- Ordinary import skips only candles whose `closeTime` is after `exchangeNow` (`MarketDataService.java:45`). It has no grace period.
- Reconciliation waits `live-close-grace (10s) + safety-margin (2s)` (`LocalCandleReconciliationService.java:127`), as AGENTS.md requires.
- Once the import closes a row, every field is frozen (`CandleRepository.java:177-188`). A later WebSocket final kline cannot correct it.
- So candle values read from REST at the close boundary become permanent. Whether Binance REST can return non-final values for a candle whose `closeTime` is at or before server time is **unknown**.

### New 2: dropped 1m and overflow closes can end with no recorded outcome
- When work backs up, the dispatcher drops a committed, tokened close in two ways:
  - **1m candles:** a newer close replaces the pending one (`PENDING_REPLACED`).
  - **Higher timeframes:** the queue holds 4 entries, and an overflow is rejected (`LANE_OVERFLOW`).
- In both cases the only trace is a log line and an in-memory counter (`CandleAnalysisDispatcher.java:74-81, 91-97`). No durable outcome is written.
- The detector is the only thing that could write a final outcome later:
  - It admits work only when the lane is idle: `idle()` at `:29`, the check at `:38`, and the detector's own check at `LocalClosedCandleDetector.java:46`.
  - It only looks back about 180 seconds (`:47-50`).
- If the lane stays busy for longer than that, the dropped candle ages out with no outcome. That breaks the AGENTS.md rule that each candle path writes one durable final outcome.

### New 3: the historical-only path is only a code branch, not a separate component
- When stale work is recovered, the worker routes it to `analyzeRecovered` (`CandleClosedAnalysisWorker.java:160-165`), so it does not trade today. That answers one possible objection.
- But the same class holds `paperTradingService` (`:37`) and calls it twenty lines later (`:175`).
- AGENTS.md asks for historical-only recovery that is *structurally unable* to reach execution. A branch inside the executing class does not meet that.

### New 4: `application.yml` declares `local-candle:` twice at the top level (lines 40 and 447)
Codex flagged this as an unknown. I add:
- There is no `@SpringBootTest` in `src/test`, and there are no test resource files. No test in the tree has ever loaded this YAML.
- From memory, not checked here: Spring Boot's YAML loader has rejected duplicate keys, and the project uses Spring Boot 4.1.0 (`pom.xml:9`). If that still holds, the checked-in configuration would fail at startup. If it doesn't, one block silently overrides the other.
- Either way, "reconciliation is off by default" still holds, because the code default is also `false` (`LocalCandleReconciliationService.java:44`).

### Correction 2: my round-5 point about the in-memory reconnect evidence was wrong
- I said `lastReconnectAdmission` stays empty after a restart until the next reconnect. Wrong: the first connection at startup also calls `requestAfterReconnect` (`BinanceWebSocketManager.java:200`; `LocalCandleReconciliationService.java:60-62`).
- I also called it "global". There is one primary session covering every symbol, so a single timestamp is appropriate.
- Still valid: it is held in memory only.
- My `LIMIT 50` objection stands unchanged (`:129-140`): the limit is shared across every symbol, has no enabled-coin filter, and is applied before the due and loss-evidence filters.

### Challenging Codex
Codex's material claims still hold:
- The duplicate-key backfill closes an open row while keeping its provenance and token.
- The detector keeps retry state in memory and only looks back a bounded window.
- Replay selects candles by `closed` and time.
- There is no Binance 429/418/`Retry-After` handling anywhere: a search of `src/main/java` found no matches.

Codex missed:
- the runtime triggers for the import race;
- the missing close grace period in ordinary import;
- dispatcher drops that end with no outcome;
- the gap between a code branch and real structural separation.

The V103/B105 and OBSERVE wording points were settled in round 4.

## OBJECTIONS (unresolved, material)
1. **Ordinary import:**
   - It closes open runtime rows without audit, outcome or provenance change, and without a close grace period.
   - Three runtime triggers (adding a coin, re-enabling one, the import endpoint) can lose a live close entirely.
2. **Dropped closes:** 1m replacement and queue overflow write no outcome, and the idle-only, lookback-bounded detector cannot guarantee one later.
3. **Reconciliation:**
   - It is off by default.
   - The `LIMIT 50` is applied before filtering.
   - `PENDING` candidates that age out get no final outcome.
   - Its retry evidence is in memory.
4. **Detector:** it writes no outcome after an outage longer than its lookback.
5. **Historical-only work:** it is separated from execution only by a code branch, not structurally.
6. **OBSERVE:** it runs both ingestion paths, and the startup import executes trades.
7. **Rate limits:** there is no shared Binance REST rate-limit or ban handling.
8. **Replay:** parity with live admission and recovery is not shown.
9. **Configuration:** the duplicate YAML key was never loaded by any test.
10. **Documentation drift:**
    - AGENTS.md calls the design "pending" and its V103 blocker text is out of date.
    - claude-docs misdescribes OBSERVE.
    - Four source comments contradict their own SQL or code.

## UNKNOWNS
- Whether the duplicate `local-candle` key breaks startup or is silently merged in Spring Boot 4.1.
- Whether Binance REST can return non-final values for a candle at the close boundary.
- Whether the import endpoint requires authentication.
- How often 1m lanes back up in practice.
- Whether `LocalCandleMySqlIT` has ever passed.
- Production artifact, mode, overrides, Flyway history, logs, and how open positions are protected during replacement.

**Candidates for a later solution review only (not authorised):**
- Make ordinary import insert-if-absent, with a close grace period, or have it skip open `RUNTIME` rows.
- Write a durable outcome when the dispatcher replaces or rejects a close.
- Move historical-only analysis into a component that has no execution dependency.
- Add a context-load test that reads the real `application.yml`.

YOUR PRIOR FINDINGS:
## FINDINGS

1. Default checked-in mode is `OFF`; in that mode the trader owns Binance WebSocket and REST candle ingestion and persists locally to `crypto_ai.candle`. `LIVE` disables those direct paths and reads candles through the configured collector datasource. No production environment was accessed.

2. `OBSERVE` is materially unsafe against the pending direction: direct WebSocket/REST ingestion is disabled only for `LIVE`, while `SharedMarketConsumer` starts its collector polling/dispatch clocks in every non-`OFF` mode. Therefore OBSERVE can operate both paths concurrently.

3. Runtime candle persistence has a real monotonic close mechanism. A runtime close gets a token only on an atomic `closed=0 → 1` update; later open updates and duplicate closes cannot overwrite the completed candle or generate another dispatch token. The close event is published only after input, protection, and observer transactions.

4. Ordinary REST bootstrap/backfill violates the pending recovery contract. Its `ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE` can finalize an existing open runtime candle, but preserves the existing provenance/token and creates neither reconciliation audit nor a `candle_analysis_outcome`. It is therefore not “insert missing identities only,” despite the source comment.

5. The reported coin-activation race is technically credible. Activation reloads the all-symbol combined WebSocket before running bootstrap. If bootstrap finalizes a just-closed, already-open runtime candle before its final WebSocket close message is processed, that later close cannot obtain a token or dispatch analysis. The detector excludes it because it has no token; reconciliation excludes it because it is no longer open. I found no test covering this interleaving.

6. A specialized reconciliation slice exists and is materially safer: it uses update-only recovery for an existing open row and insert-if-absent for a wholly absent candle, records audit/outcome evidence, and never dispatches recovered work. But it is disabled by the checked-in configuration, so it is not the current active default behavior.

7. Reconciliation still has unproven/material weaknesses even if enabled:
   - It takes `LIMIT 50` per interval before due-status and loss-evidence filtering.
   - It does not restrict open candidates to enabled symbols.
   - Reconnect loss evidence is process-local (`lastReconnectAdmission`) and disappears across restart.
   - Retryable candidates age out of the quality window without a demonstrated terminal age-out outcome.
   - REST clients have bounded timeouts but no shared 429/418, `Retry-After`, ban, or global request-budget coordination.

8. The detector addresses the commit-to-dispatch crash window only while a tokened candle remains inside its bounded lookback. Retry state is in memory; after a sufficiently long outage, an old unanalysed tokened candle falls outside SQL selection without a detector-created terminal outcome. This is confirmed by code and even directly reflected in the unit test that asserts old runtime candles are not analysed.

9. Replay reads closed local/selected-source candles by symbol, interval, time, and `closed=true`. It does not classify provenance, close token, reconciliation audit, outcome, or “historical-only” recovery state. It provides narrower price-event checks, not demonstrated parity for candle recovery/admission behavior.

10. Documentation has material drift:
    - AGENTS.md and `codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` call V103 an unconditional fresh trader-only blocker, but `B105` expressly establishes a trader-only baseline intended to bypass V103; a source-level resolution test exists. A real disposable-MySQL success report was not found, so this corrects the categorical claim without proving deployment readiness.
    - `claude-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` incorrectly groups OBSERVE with LIVE/shared-only operation; code allows direct ingestion in OBSERVE.
    - `MarketDataBootstrapService` and `CandleRepository` comments claim missing-only backfill, contradicted by their implementation.

## OBJECTIONS

- Default OFF-mode bootstrap can silently consume a live close without analysis/outcome evidence.
- Default reconciliation is disabled; enabled reconciliation still lacks demonstrated fair candidate selection, durable restart evidence, terminal ageing, and coordinated Binance rate-limit handling.
- OBSERVE permits direct and shared-market ingestion together, contrary to the pending architecture.
- Replay parity does not cover the implemented recovery/admission classifications.
- Required MySQL, crash/restart, concurrency, replay, and deployment-replacement proof is absent.

Candidate for later solution review only: separate ordinary historical import from authority to finalize an existing runtime-open row, then test the activation/reconnect boundary against disposable MySQL.

## UNKNOWNS

- Production artifact, overrides, mode, database/Flyway history, collector availability, and actual operational behavior were intentionally not accessed.
- Whether the activation race has occurred in reality.
- Whether `LocalCandleMySqlIT` has passed on disposable MySQL; the source test exists, but no saved report was found.
- Whether every administrative coin mutation invokes stream reload; the documented reload paths do, but no runtime audit was inspected.
- Open-position protection coverage during application replacement.

### src/main/java/com/crypto/repository/CandleRepository.java
   1: package com.crypto.repository;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.Candle;
   4: import com.crypto.shared.SharedMarketSource;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;
   6: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
   7: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.RowMapper;
   8: import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
   9: import java.math.BigDecimal;
  10: import java.sql.Timestamp;
  11: import java.time.Instant;
  12: import java.util.*;

  14: /** FIX-153: local candle facade in OFF; shared reads remain a rollback capability.
  15:  * Exact predicates/order are retained. Candle is a DTO; SQL migrations own its schema.
  16:  * Runtime and backfill write policies are deliberately distinct. */
  17: @Repository
  18: public class CandleRepository {
  19:     private static final org.slf4j.Logger log=org.slf4j.LoggerFactory.getLogger(CandleRepository.class);
  20:     private final SharedMarketSource source;
  21:     private final JdbcTemplate local;
  22:     public CandleRepository(SharedMarketSource source, JdbcTemplate local) { this.source=source; this.local=local; }
  23:     public static final RowMapper<Candle> MAPPER = (r,n) -> Candle.builder()
  24:         .id(r.getLong("id")).symbol(r.getString("symbol")).intervalCode(r.getString("interval_code"))
  25:         .openTime(r.getTimestamp("open_time").toInstant()).closeTime(r.getTimestamp("close_time").toInstant())
  26:         .openPrice(r.getBigDecimal("open_price")).highPrice(r.getBigDecimal("high_price"))
  27:         .lowPrice(r.getBigDecimal("low_price")).closePrice(r.getBigDecimal("close_price"))
  28:         .volume(r.getBigDecimal("volume")).quoteAssetVolume(r.getBigDecimal("quote_asset_volume"))
  29:         .numberOfTrades(r.getObject("number_of_trades",Long.class))
  30:         .takerBuyBaseVolume(r.getBigDecimal("taker_buy_base_volume"))
  31:         .takerBuyQuoteVolume(r.getBigDecimal("taker_buy_quote_volume")).closed(r.getBoolean("closed")).build();
  32:     private List<Candle> read(String tail,Object... args) {
  33:         Object[] converted=Arrays.stream(args).map(v->v instanceof Instant i ? Timestamp.from(i) : v).toArray();
  34:         List<Candle> rows=source.reader().query("SELECT * FROM candle WHERE "+tail,MAPPER,converted);
  35:         com.crypto.shared.CandleInputAudit.capture(tail,rows);
  36:         return rows;
  37:     }
  38:     private String page(Pageable p) { return p.isUnpaged()? "" : " LIMIT "+p.getPageSize()+" OFFSET "+p.getOffset(); }
  39:     public Optional<Candle> findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndOpenTime(String s,String i,Instant t) {
  40:         return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time=?",s,i,t).stream().findFirst();
  41:     }
  42:     public List<Candle> findTop500BySymbolAndIntervalCodeOrderByOpenTimeDesc(String s,String i) {
  43:         return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? ORDER BY open_time DESC LIMIT 500",s,i);
  44:     }
  45:     public List<Candle> findTop200BySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueOrderByOpenTimeDesc(String s,String i) {
  46:         return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 ORDER BY open_time DESC LIMIT 200",s,i);
  47:     }
  48:     public List<Candle> findClosedCandles(String s,String i,Pageable p) {
  49:         return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 ORDER BY open_time DESC"+page(p),s,i);
  50:     }
  51:     public List<Candle> findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(String s,String i,Instant t,Pageable p) {
  52:         return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 AND open_time<=? ORDER BY open_time DESC"+page(p),s,i,t);
  53:     }
  54:     public List<Candle> findClosedCandlesClosedAtOrBefore(String s,String i,Instant t,Pageable p) {
  55:         return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 AND close_time<=? ORDER BY open_time DESC"+page(p),s,i,t);
  56:     }
  57:     public List<Candle> findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(String s,String i,Instant a,Instant b) {
  58:         return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 AND open_time BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY open_time ASC",s,i,a,b);
  59:     }
  60:     public List<Candle> findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(String s,String i,Instant a,Instant b) {
  61:         return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY open_time ASC",s,i,a,b);
  62:     }
  63:     public List<Candle> findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueOrderByOpenTimeAsc(String s,String i) {
  64:         return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 ORDER BY open_time ASC",s,i);
  65:     }
  66:     public Optional<Candle> findFirstBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueOrderByCloseTimeDesc(String s,String i) {
  67:         return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 ORDER BY close_time DESC LIMIT 1",s,i).stream().findFirst();
  68:     }
  69:     public Optional<Candle> findFirstBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueOrderByOpenTimeAsc(String s,String i) {
  70:         return read("symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 ORDER BY open_time ASC LIMIT 1",s,i).stream().findFirst();
  71:     }
  72:     public List<String> findDistinctSymbols() { return source.reader().queryForList("SELECT DISTINCT symbol FROM candle ORDER BY symbol",String.class); }
  73:     public long countBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrue(String s,String i) {
  74:         return source.reader().queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1",Long.class,s,i);
  75:     }
  76:     public List<Candle> findClosedCandlesMissingAnalysisThrough(String s,String i,Instant a,Instant b,Pageable p) {
  77:         // Shared credentials never need access to Trader's indicators/signals. Fetch
  78:         // local identities separately, then paginate AFTER applying the missing test.
  79:         Set<Instant> indicators=new HashSet<>(local.query("SELECT candle_open_time FROM technical_indicator WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time BETWEEN ? AND ?",
  80:             (r,n)->r.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),s,i,Timestamp.from(a),Timestamp.from(b)));
  81:         Set<Instant> signals=new HashSet<>(local.query("SELECT candle_open_time FROM trade_signal WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time BETWEEN ? AND ?",
  82:             (r,n)->r.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),s,i,Timestamp.from(a),Timestamp.from(b)));
  83:         long started=System.nanoTime();
  84:         var candles=findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(s,i,a,b);
  85:         long fetchMs=(System.nanoTime()-started)/1_000_000;
  86:         var result=candles.stream()
  87:             .filter(c->!indicators.contains(c.getOpenTime()) || !signals.contains(c.getOpenTime()))
  88:             .skip(p.isUnpaged()?0:p.getOffset()).limit(p.isUnpaged()?Long.MAX_VALUE:p.getPageSize()).toList();
  89:         // Measurement only. Fetch includes pool acquisition, transfer and mapping;
  90:         // it is not labelled database execution or commit time. Filtering unchanged.
  91:         if(source.enabled()) {
  92:             String detail="fetched="+candles.size()+", returned="+result.size()+", fetchIncludingPoolMs="+fetchMs+", pool="+source.poolPressure();
  93:             log.info("[FIX-132][RECOVERY_SCAN] symbol={}, interval={}, from={}, through={}, {}",s,i,a,b,detail);
  94:             try { local.update("INSERT INTO shared_market_health(component,status,detail) VALUES(?,'MEASURED',?) ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE status=VALUES(status),detail=VALUES(detail)","RECOVERY:"+s+":"+i,detail); }
  95:             catch(RuntimeException unavailable) {log.warn("[FIX-132][RECOVERY_METRIC_NOT_PERSISTED] symbol={}, interval={}",s,i,unavailable);}
  96:         }
  97:         return result;
  98:     }
  99:     public int upsert(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
 100:         BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
 101:         BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
 102:         BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume,boolean closed) {
 103:         source.requireLocalWriter();
 104:         return write(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,closeTime,openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,closePrice,
 105:             volume,quoteAssetVolume,numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,closed,"BACKFILL",null);
 106:     }
 107:     /** FIX-153: result is valid only after the caller's INPUT transaction commits.
 108:      * Token read-back compares immutable receipt identity, never a racy pre-read of closed state. */
 109:     public Transition upsertRuntime(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
 110:         BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
 111:         BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
 112:         BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume,boolean closed,String token) {
 113:         source.requireLocalWriter();
 114:         write(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,closeTime,openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,closePrice,
 115:             volume,quoteAssetVolume,numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,closed,"RUNTIME",closed?token:null);
 116:         return local.queryForObject("SELECT closed,close_time,close_transition_token,ingestion_source FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time=?",
 117:             // Provenance deliberately does not participate here. A legacy NULL row that
 118:             // the live stream closes is a genuine close transition; BACKFILL never writes
 119:             // a token, so it cannot manufacture a dispatchable transition.
 120:             (rs,n)->new Transition(closed && token.equals(rs.getString(3)),
 121:                 rs.getTimestamp(2).toInstant(),rs.getString(3),rs.getBoolean(1)),symbol,intervalCode,Timestamp.from(openTime));
 122:     }
 123:     /** `alreadyClosed` is diagnostic only; the matching token is the sole dispatch authority. */
 124:     public record Transition(boolean transitionedToClosed,Instant closeTime,String token,boolean alreadyClosed) {}
 125: 
 126:     /**
 127:      * Authoritative recovery is intentionally update-only.  Unlike ordinary
 128:      * REST backfill it can never insert an absent identity and it never makes a
 129:      * runtime close token.  This keeps a late recovery from stealing a normal
 130:      * live close admission.
 131:      */
 132:     public boolean finalizeExistingOpenFromAuthoritative(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
 133:         BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
 134:         BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
 135:         BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume) {
 136:         source.requireLocalWriter();
 137:         return local.update("""
 138:             UPDATE candle SET close_time=?,open_price=?,high_price=?,low_price=?,close_price=?,volume=?,
 139:               quote_asset_volume=?,number_of_trades=?,taker_buy_base_volume=?,taker_buy_quote_volume=?,
 140:               closed=1,updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 141:             WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time=? AND closed=0
 142:               AND (ingestion_source IS NULL OR ingestion_source<>'BACKFILL')
 143:             """, Timestamp.from(closeTime),openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,closePrice,volume,quoteAssetVolume,
 144:             numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,symbol,intervalCode,Timestamp.from(openTime)) == 1;
 145:     }
 146: 
 147:     /**
 148:      * Exact authoritative gap repair is pure insert-if-absent.  Do not replace
 149:      * this with ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE: a live row may arrive while REST is in
 150:      * flight and must remain untouched so its normal close can own the token.
 151:      */
 152:     public boolean insertAuthoritativeGapBackfill(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
 153:         BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
 154:         BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
 155:         BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume) {
 156:         source.requireLocalWriter();
 157:         try {
 158:             return local.update("""
 159:                 INSERT INTO candle(symbol,interval_code,open_time,close_time,open_price,high_price,low_price,close_price,
 160:                     volume,quote_asset_volume,number_of_trades,taker_buy_base_volume,taker_buy_quote_volume,
 161:                     closed,ingestion_source,close_transition_token,created_at,updated_at)
 162:                 VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,1,'BACKFILL',NULL,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6))
 163:                 """,symbol,intervalCode,Timestamp.from(openTime),Timestamp.from(closeTime),openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,
 164:                 closePrice,volume,quoteAssetVolume,numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume) == 1;
 165:         } catch (org.springframework.dao.DuplicateKeyException duplicate) {
 166:             return false;
 167:         }
 168:     }
 169:     private int write(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
 170:         BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
 171:         BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
 172:         BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume,boolean closed,String provenance,String token) {
 173:         String duplicateClause = "BACKFILL".equals(provenance) ? """
 174:             ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
 175:                 -- REST/backfill is authoritative enough to finish an interrupted open
 176:                 -- candle, but never changes a completed candle, provenance or token.
 177:                 close_time=IF(closed=1,close_time,VALUES(close_time)),
 178:                 open_price=IF(closed=1,open_price,VALUES(open_price)),
 179:                 high_price=IF(closed=1,high_price,VALUES(high_price)),
 180:                 low_price=IF(closed=1,low_price,VALUES(low_price)),
 181:                 close_price=IF(closed=1,close_price,VALUES(close_price)),
 182:                 volume=IF(closed=1,volume,VALUES(volume)),
 183:                 quote_asset_volume=IF(closed=1,quote_asset_volume,VALUES(quote_asset_volume)),
 184:                 number_of_trades=IF(closed=1,number_of_trades,VALUES(number_of_trades)),
 185:                 taker_buy_base_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_base_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_base_volume)),
 186:                 taker_buy_quote_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_quote_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_quote_volume)),
 187:                 updated_at=IF(closed=1,updated_at,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)),
 188:                 closed=GREATEST(closed,VALUES(closed))
 189:             """ : """
 190:             ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
 191:                 close_time=IF(closed=1,close_time,VALUES(close_time)),
 192:                 open_price=IF(closed=1,open_price,VALUES(open_price)),
 193:                 high_price=IF(closed=1,high_price,VALUES(high_price)),
 194:                 low_price=IF(closed=1,low_price,VALUES(low_price)),
 195:                 close_price=IF(closed=1,close_price,VALUES(close_price)),
 196:                 volume=IF(closed=1,volume,VALUES(volume)),
 197:                 quote_asset_volume=IF(closed=1,quote_asset_volume,VALUES(quote_asset_volume)),
 198:                 number_of_trades=IF(closed=1,number_of_trades,VALUES(number_of_trades)),
 199:                 taker_buy_base_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_base_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_base_volume)),
 200:                 taker_buy_quote_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_quote_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_quote_volume)),
 201:                 close_transition_token=IF(closed=0 AND VALUES(closed)=1,VALUES(close_transition_token),close_transition_token),
 202:                 updated_at=IF(closed=1,updated_at,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)),
 203:                 closed=GREATEST(closed,VALUES(closed))
 204:             """;
 205:         // FIX-153: backfill inserts missing identities only, so it cannot fabricate a runtime close.
 206:         return local.update("""
 207:             INSERT INTO candle(symbol,interval_code,open_time,close_time,open_price,high_price,low_price,close_price,
 208:                 volume,quote_asset_volume,number_of_trades,taker_buy_base_volume,taker_buy_quote_volume,
 209:                 closed,ingestion_source,close_transition_token,created_at,updated_at)
 210:             VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6))
 211:             """ + duplicateClause,symbol,intervalCode,Timestamp.from(openTime),Timestamp.from(closeTime),openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,
 212:             closePrice,volume,quoteAssetVolume,numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,closed,provenance,token);
 213:     }
 214: }

### src/main/java/com/crypto/service/MarketDataService.java
   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import java.util.Comparator;
   4: import java.util.List;
   5: import java.util.Locale;
   6: 
   7: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   8: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
   9: import org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager;
  10: import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate;
  11: 
  12: import com.crypto.client.binance.BinanceMarketDataClient;
  13: import com.crypto.client.binance.dto.BinanceKline;
  14: import com.crypto.domain.Candle;
  15: import com.crypto.repository.CandleRepository;
  16: 
  17: @Service
  18: public class MarketDataService {
  19:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("#{'${shared-market.mode:OFF}' == 'LIVE'}")
  20:     private boolean sharedMarketEnabled;
  21: 
  22: 
  23:     private final BinanceMarketDataClient client;
  24:     private final CandleRepository candleRepository;
  25:     private final TransactionTemplate transaction;
  26: 
  27:     public MarketDataService(BinanceMarketDataClient client,CandleRepository candleRepository,PlatformTransactionManager manager){
  28:         this.client=client;this.candleRepository=candleRepository;this.transaction=new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  29:     }
  30: 
  31:     public int importCandles(String symbol, String interval, int limit) {
  32:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132: collector owns candle imports"); }
  33:         String normalizedSymbol = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  34:         // Do not trust a potentially skewed local clock to finalise Binance's
  35:         // currently-open REST candle. Read it before klines: a later timestamp
  36:         // could otherwise turn an in-flight response row into a false close.
  37:         java.time.Instant exchangeNow=client.getServerTime();
  38:         List<BinanceKline> klines = client.getKlines(normalizedSymbol, interval, limit);
  39:         // No database transaction is open while Binance is contacted. The local
  40:         // writes remain one transaction once the bounded network calls return.
  41:         Integer upserted=transaction.execute(status->{
  42:             int count=0;
  43:             for (BinanceKline kline : klines) {
  44:                 // FIX-153: an in-progress REST candle is not a close event or historical evidence.
  45:                 if (kline.closeTime().isAfter(exchangeNow)) continue;
  46:                 count += candleRepository.upsert(
  47:                         normalizedSymbol,interval,kline.openTime(),kline.closeTime(),
  48:                         kline.openPrice(),kline.highPrice(),kline.lowPrice(),kline.closePrice(),
  49:                         kline.volume(),kline.quoteAssetVolume(),kline.numberOfTrades(),
  50:                         kline.takerBuyBaseVolume(),kline.takerBuyQuoteVolume(),true);
  51:             }
  52:             return count;
  53:         });
  54:         if(upserted==null)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 local candle import transaction returned no result");
  55:         return upserted;
  56:     }
  57: 
  58:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
  59:     public List<Candle> latestCandles(String symbol, String interval) {
  60:         return candleRepository
  61:                 .findTop500BySymbolAndIntervalCodeOrderByOpenTimeDesc(
  62:                         symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT), interval)
  63:                 .stream()
  64:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(Candle::getOpenTime))
  65:                 .toList();
  66:     }
  67: 
  68:     private Candle toEntity(String symbol, String interval, BinanceKline kline) {
  69:         return Candle.builder()
  70:                 .symbol(symbol)
  71:                 .intervalCode(interval)
  72:                 .openTime(kline.openTime())
  73:                 .closeTime(kline.closeTime())
  74:                 .openPrice(kline.openPrice())
  75:                 .highPrice(kline.highPrice())
  76:                 .lowPrice(kline.lowPrice())
  77:                 .closePrice(kline.closePrice())
  78:                 .volume(kline.volume())
  79:                 .quoteAssetVolume(kline.quoteAssetVolume())
  80:                 .numberOfTrades(kline.numberOfTrades())
  81:                 .takerBuyBaseVolume(kline.takerBuyBaseVolume())
  82:                 .takerBuyQuoteVolume(kline.takerBuyQuoteVolume())
  83:                 .closed(true)
  84:                 .build();
  85:     }
  86: }

### src/main/java/com/crypto/client/binance/websocket/BinanceWebSocketManager.java
   1: package com.crypto.client.binance.websocket;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.client.config.binance.BinanceMarketDataProperties;
   4: import com.crypto.service.BinanceKlineService;
   5: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
   6: import jakarta.annotation.PreDestroy;
   7: import java.net.URI;
   8: import java.time.Instant;
   9: import java.util.ArrayList;
  10: import java.util.List;
  11: import java.util.concurrent.*;
  12: import java.util.concurrent.locks.LockSupport;
  13: import java.util.function.Supplier;
  14: import org.slf4j.Logger;
  15: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
  16: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
  17: import org.springframework.boot.context.event.ApplicationReadyEvent;
  18: import org.springframework.context.event.EventListener;
  19: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
  20: import org.springframework.web.socket.WebSocketSession;
  21: import org.springframework.web.socket.client.standard.StandardWebSocketClient;
  22: 
  23: /** FIX-131: one owner for startup/reload/health. The actor never does database or socket I/O.
  24:  * A timed-out, unresolved handshake is retained: cancellation alone cannot prove no late socket.
  25:  * Availability is deliberately sacrificed until old work AND transport have terminated. */
  26: @Component
  27: public class BinanceWebSocketManager {
  28:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("#{'${shared-market.mode:OFF}' == 'LIVE'}")
  29:     private boolean sharedMarketEnabled;
  30: 
  31:     private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(BinanceWebSocketManager.class);
  32:     static final long SCAN_NANOS = TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS.toNanos(500);
  33:     static final long DEADLINE_NANOS = TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(15);
  34:     interface Connector { CompletableFuture<WebSocketSession> connect(BinanceWebSocketHandler handler, String url); }
  35:     private final BinanceMarketDataProperties properties;
  36:     private final Supplier<String> urls;
  37:     private final ObjectMapper mapper;
  38:     private final BinanceKlineService service;
  39:     private final Connector connector;
  40:     private final CandleGapDiagnostics gaps;
  41:     @Autowired(required=false) private com.crypto.local.LocalCandleReconciliationService reconciliation;
  42:     private final java.util.function.LongSupplier nanos;
  43:     private final ArrayBlockingQueue<CompletableFuture<Void>> requests = new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(32);
  44:     private final ThreadPoolExecutor connectWorker = worker("fix131-connect", 1);
  45:     private final ThreadPoolExecutor closeWorker = worker("fix131-close", 1);
  46:     private final Object lifecycle = new Object();
  47:     private final CompletableFuture<Void> stopped = new CompletableFuture<>();
  48:     private final List<CompletableFuture<Void>> waiting = new ArrayList<>();
  49:     private final java.util.Set<BinanceWebSocketHandler> cleanup = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  50:     private final java.util.Set<BinanceWebSocketHandler> cleaning = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  51:     private volatile boolean stopRequested;
  52:     private volatile Thread actor;
  53:     private volatile String phase = "STOPPED";
  54:     private volatile Attempt current;
  55:     private long sequence;
  56:     private long interruptionStart;
  57:     private Instant revokedAt;
  58:     private long nextAttempt;
  59:     private boolean replacement;
  60:     private static final class Attempt {
  61:         final BinanceWebSocketHandler handler;
  62:         final long started;
  63:         volatile boolean settled;
  64:         volatile Throwable failure;
  65:         volatile String url;
  66:         volatile boolean closing;
  67:         long nextClose;
  68:         long revoked;
  69:         boolean timeoutLogged;
  70:         long nextSilenceLog;
  71:         Attempt(BinanceWebSocketHandler handler, long started) { this.handler = handler; this.started = started; }
  72:     }
  73:     @Autowired
  74:     public BinanceWebSocketManager(BinanceMarketDataProperties properties, BinanceStreamUrlBuilder builder,
  75:             ObjectMapper mapper, BinanceKlineService service, CandleGapDiagnostics gaps) {
  76:         this(properties, builder::build, mapper, service,
  77:                 (handler, url) -> new StandardWebSocketClient().execute(handler, null, URI.create(url)), gaps);
  78:     }
  79:     BinanceWebSocketManager(BinanceMarketDataProperties properties, Supplier<String> urls,
  80:             ObjectMapper mapper, BinanceKlineService service, Connector connector, CandleGapDiagnostics gaps) {
  81:         this(properties, urls, mapper, service, connector, gaps, System::nanoTime);
  82:     }
  83:     BinanceWebSocketManager(BinanceMarketDataProperties properties, Supplier<String> urls,
  84:             ObjectMapper mapper, BinanceKlineService service, Connector connector, CandleGapDiagnostics gaps,
  85:             java.util.function.LongSupplier nanos) {
  86:         this.nanos = nanos;
  87:         this.properties = properties; this.urls = urls; this.mapper = mapper; this.service = service;
  88:         this.connector = connector; this.gaps = gaps;
  89:     }
  90:     private static ThreadPoolExecutor worker(String name, int queue) {
  91:         return new ThreadPoolExecutor(1, 1, 0, TimeUnit.SECONDS, new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(queue), task -> {
  92:             Thread t = new Thread(task, name); t.setDaemon(true); return t;
  93:         }, new ThreadPoolExecutor.AbortPolicy());
  94:     }
  95:     @EventListener(ApplicationReadyEvent.class)
  96:     public void start() {
  97:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
  98:         synchronized (lifecycle) {
  99:             if (actor != null || stopRequested || !properties.getWebsocket().isEnabled()) return;
 100:             replacement = true;
 101:             actor = new Thread(this::run, "fix131-ws-owner");
 102:             actor.setDaemon(true);
 103:             actor.start();
 104:         }
 105:     }
 106:     void wake() { LockSupport.unpark(actor); }
 107:     private void notice(BinanceWebSocketHandler handler) {
 108:         if (handler.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.REVOKED && !handler.allClosed()) {
 109:             if (current == null || current.handler != handler) cleanup.add(handler);
 110:             // After bounded shutdown, even the unresolved CURRENT handshake retains a cleanup path.
 111:             // This fallback runs only during shutdown, never on a live processing callback.
 112:             if (closeWorker.isShutdown() && handler.gate.isDrained()) handler.close();
 113:         }
 114:         wake();
 115:     }
 116:     CompletableFuture<Void> requestReload() {
 117:         if (!properties.getWebsocket().isEnabled()) throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.CONNECTION_FAILED);
 118:         var request = new CompletableFuture<Void>();
 119:         synchronized (lifecycle) {
 120:             if (stopRequested) throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.STOPPED);
 121:             if (!requests.offer(request)) throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.BUSY);
 122:         }
 123:         start(); wake();
 124:         return request;
 125:     }
 126:     public void reload() {
 127:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
 128:         var request = requestReload();
 129:         try { request.get(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS); }
 130:         catch (TimeoutException ex) { throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.WAIT_TIMEOUT); }
 131:         catch (InterruptedException ex) { Thread.currentThread().interrupt(); throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.WAIT_TIMEOUT); }
 132:         catch (ExecutionException ex) {
 133:             if (ex.getCause() instanceof RuntimeException runtime) throw runtime;
 134:             throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.CONNECTION_FAILED);
 135:         }
 136:     }
 137:     private WebSocketReconnectException failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason reason) {
 138:         return new WebSocketReconnectException(reason, phase);
 139:     }
 140:     private void run() {
 141:         try {
 142:             while (true) {
 143:                 // Persistent lifecycle facts are examined on EVERY pass, before more queued requests.
 144:                 tick();
 145:                 if (stopRequested && current == null && cleanup.isEmpty()) break;
 146:                 LockSupport.parkNanos(SCAN_NANOS);
 147:             }
 148:             phase = "STOPPED";
 149:             finish(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.STOPPED);
 150:             stopped.complete(null);
 151:         } catch (Throwable ex) {
 152:             log.error("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][OWNER_FAILED] phase={}; restart required", phase, ex);
 153:             synchronized (lifecycle) { stopRequested = true; if (current != null) current.handler.gate.revoke(); }
 154:             finish(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.STOPPED);
 155:             stopped.completeExceptionally(ex);
 156:         }
 157:     }
 158:     private void tick() {
 159:         long now = nanos.getAsLong();
 160:         for (var h : cleanup) {
 161:             if (h.allClosed()) { cleanup.remove(h); continue; }
 162:             if (h.gate.isDrained() && cleaning.add(h)) {
 163:                 try { closeWorker.execute(() -> {
 164:                     try { h.close(); } finally { cleaning.remove(h); wake(); }
 165:                 }); } catch (RejectedExecutionException ex) { cleaning.remove(h); }
 166:             }
 167:         }
 168:         // Bound callers retained off-mailbox as well as mailbox capacity.
 169:         CompletableFuture<Void> request;
 170:         for (int n = 0; n < 32 && (request = requests.poll()) != null; n++) {
 171:             if (waiting.size() >= 32) request.completeExceptionally(failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.BUSY));
 172:             else { waiting.add(request); replacement = true; }
 173:         }
 174:         if (current != null) {
 175:             var a = current;
 176:             var h = a.handler;
 177:             if (a.failure != null && h.gate.state() != GenerationGate.State.REVOKED) {
 178:                 log.error("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][CONNECTION_FAILED] generation={}", h.generation, a.failure);
 179:                 finish(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.CONNECTION_FAILED);
 180:             }
 181:             if (stopRequested || replacement || h.failed || a.failure != null
 182:                     || (h.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.ADMITTING && !h.isConnected())) revoke(a, now);
 183:             if (h.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.PENDING) {
 184:                 if (now - a.started >= DEADLINE_NANOS) {
 185:                     log.error("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][CONNECTION_TIMEOUT] generation={}; awaiting terminal handshake/closure", h.generation);
 186:                     revoke(a, now);
 187:                     finish(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.CONNECTION_FAILED);
 188:                 } else if (a.settled && h.isConnected()) {
 189:                     synchronized (lifecycle) {
 190:                         if (!stopRequested && h.gate.activate()) {
 191:                             phase = "ACTIVE";
 192:                             h.lastReceiptNanos = System.nanoTime();
 193:                             log.info("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][ADMISSION_ENABLED] generation={}, admittedAt={}, revokedAt={}, interruptionMs={}",
 194:                                     h.generation, Instant.now(), revokedAt, interruptionStart == 0 ? 0 : TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.toMillis(now - interruptionStart));
 195:                             interruptionStart = 0;
 196:                             try { if (gaps != null) gaps.subscribed(a.url, Instant.now()); }
 197:                             catch (RuntimeException ex) { log.warn("[FIX-131][SUBSCRIPTION_DIAGNOSTIC_FAILED] generation={}", h.generation, ex); }
 198:                             // Request only; all local queries and REST recovery run on
 199:                             // the dedicated reconciliation executor, never this actor.
 200:                             try { if(reconciliation!=null) reconciliation.requestAfterReconnect(h.generation); }
 201:                             catch(RuntimeException ex) { log.warn("[FIX-153][RECONCILIATION_REQUEST_FAILED] generation={}",h.generation,ex); }
 202:                             finish(null);
 203:                         }
 204:                     }
 205:                 }
 206:             }
 207:             if (h.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.ADMITTING && now >= a.nextSilenceLog && System.nanoTime() - h.lastReceiptNanos > TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(30)) {
 208:                 a.nextSilenceLog = now + TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(30);
 209:                 log.warn("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][NO_MESSAGES] generation={}, silenceMs={}; observe only, no forced reconnect", h.generation, TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.toMillis(System.nanoTime() - h.lastReceiptNanos));
 210:             }
 211:             if (h.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.REVOKED) {
 212:                 phase = h.gate.isDrained() ? "CLOSING" : "DRAINING";
 213:                 if (now - a.revoked >= DEADLINE_NANOS && !a.timeoutLogged) {
 214:                     a.timeoutLogged = true;
 215:                     var reason = h.gate.isDrained() ? WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.CLOSE_TIMEOUT : WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.DRAIN_TIMEOUT;
 216:                     log.error("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][{}] generation={}, inFlight={}; replacement remains blocked", reason, h.generation, h.gate.inFlight());
 217:                     finish(reason);
 218:                 }
 219:                 if (h.gate.isDrained()) {
 220:                     if (!h.allClosed() && !a.closing && now >= a.nextClose) {
 221:                         a.closing = true;
 222:                         try { closeWorker.execute(() -> { try { h.close(); } finally { a.closing = false; wake(); } }); }
 223:                         catch (RejectedExecutionException ex) { a.closing = false; log.error("[FIX-131][CLOSE_WORKER_REJECTED] generation={}", h.generation); }
 224:                         a.nextClose = now + TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(5);
 225:                     }
 226:                     if (a.settled && h.allClosed() && !a.closing) {
 227:                         log.info("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][RETIRED] generation={}, drainedAt={}, rejectedMessages={}", h.generation, h.gate.drainedAt(), h.rejected.get());
 228:                         current = null;
 229:                         replacement = !stopRequested;
 230:                         nextAttempt = now + (a.failure == null ? 0 : TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(Math.max(1, properties.getWebsocket().getHealthCheckSeconds())));
 231:                     }
 232:                 }
 233:             }
 234:         }
 235:         synchronized (lifecycle) {
 236:             if (!stopRequested && current == null && cleanup.isEmpty() && replacement && now >= nextAttempt) launch();
 237:         }
 238:     }
 239:     private void revoke(Attempt a, long now) {
 240:         if (a.handler.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.REVOKED) return;
 241:         a.handler.gate.revoke();
 242:         a.revoked = now;
 243:         if (interruptionStart == 0) { interruptionStart = now; revokedAt = Instant.now(); }
 244:         log.warn("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][REVOKED] generation={}, inFlight={}", a.handler.generation, a.handler.gate.inFlight());
 245:     }
 246:     private void launch() {
 247:         replacement = false;
 248:         phase = "PENDING";
 249:         Attempt a = new Attempt(new BinanceWebSocketHandler(mapper, service, ++sequence, this::notice), nanos.getAsLong());
 250:         current = a;
 251:         log.info("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][CONNECTING] generation={}", sequence);
 252:         try {
 253:             connectWorker.execute(() -> {
 254:                 try {
 255:                     a.url = urls.get(); // DB-backed subscription snapshot is never loaded by actor.
 256:                     synchronized (lifecycle) {
 257:                         if (stopRequested || a.handler.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.REVOKED) {
 258:                             a.settled = true; wake(); return;
 259:                         }
 260:                         // execute returns a future; no waiting under the lifecycle lock.
 261:                         connector.connect(a.handler, a.url).whenComplete((session, ex) -> {
 262:                             if (session != null && a.handler.primary != session) a.handler.afterConnectionEstablished(session);
 263:                             a.failure = ex; a.settled = true; wake();
 264:                         });
 265:                     }
 266:                 } catch (Throwable ex) { a.failure = ex; a.settled = true; wake(); }
 267:             });
 268:         } catch (RejectedExecutionException ex) { a.failure = ex; a.settled = true; }
 269:     }
 270:     private void finish(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason reason) {
 271:         for (var f : waiting) { if (reason == null) f.complete(null); else f.completeExceptionally(failure(reason)); }
 272:         waiting.clear();
 273:     }
 274:     boolean isStopRequested() { return stopRequested; }
 275:     public String lifecyclePhase() { return phase; }
 276:     @PreDestroy public void stop() {
 277:         synchronized (lifecycle) { stopRequested = true; }
 278:         wake();
 279:         if (actor != null) try { stopped.get(20, TimeUnit.SECONDS); }
 280:         catch (Exception ex) { log.error("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][SHUTDOWN_INCOMPLETE] phase={}; cleanup not confirmed", phase); }
 281:         connectWorker.shutdown(); closeWorker.shutdown();
 282:     }
 283: }

### src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketConsumer.java
   1: package com.crypto.shared;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.administration.service.CoinConfigurationService;
   4: import com.crypto.indicator.event.CandleClosedAnalysisWorker;
   5: import com.crypto.position.service.LivePositionProtectionService;
   6: import com.crypto.debug.monitor.service.PriceMoveMonitorService;
   7: import jakarta.annotation.PreDestroy;
   8: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   9: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
  10: import org.springframework.transaction.*;
  11: import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate;
  12: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Qualifier;
  13: import org.slf4j.*;
  14: import java.sql.Timestamp;
  15: import java.time.Instant;
  16: import java.util.*;
  17: import java.util.concurrent.*;
  18: 
  19: /** FIX-132 independent consumer. The immutable source is never claimed/updated.
  20:  * Each phase fences on the local symbol row through COMMIT, then releases all
  21:  * locks before the next phase. Unknown analysis outcomes are never auto-retried.
  22:  * Durable rows are the backlog; executors never hold an unbounded event queue. */
  23: @Component
  24: public class SharedMarketConsumer {

  40:     private volatile boolean stopped;

  44:         task->new Thread(task,"fix146-readiness"));
  45:     private final JdbcTemplate readinessJdbc;

  47:     private final SharedAutomaticActivation automaticActivation;
  48:     // FIX-148: deployment policy authorizes evidence-based automatic FIRST cutover.
  49:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.automatic-first-cutover-enabled:true}")
  50:     private boolean automaticFirstCutoverEnabled;
  51:     private int readinessCursor;
  52:     private volatile List<String> readinessSymbols=List.of();
  53:     // FIX-142 flags remain off until migration, legacy-owner inventory and MySQL acceptance pass.
  54:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.fix142.enabled:false}")
  55:     private boolean fix142Enabled;
  56:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.fix142.acknowledgment-enabled:false}")
  57:     private boolean acknowledgmentEnabled;
  58:     // FIX-150: mandatory in Spring, null only in constructor-only tests. Recovery
  59:     // owns a separate transaction; invoke only outside a held symbol lock.
  60:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired
  61:     private Fix150ProtectionRecovery protectionRecovery;
  62:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.analysis.historical-threads:1}")

  64:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.fix142.admission-ceiling:32}")

  66:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.fix142.fresh-reserve:4}")
  67:     private int freshReserve=4;
  68:     // Only a known pre-business deferral may reuse this process-local attempt token.
  69:     private final Map<Long,String> deferredProtection=new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
  70:     private final Fix142DeliveryProtocol protocol;
  71:     private final ScheduledExecutorService protectionClock=Executors.newSingleThreadScheduledExecutor(r->new Thread(r,"fix142-protection"));
  72:     private final ScheduledExecutorService freshClock=Executors.newSingleThreadScheduledExecutor(r->new Thread(r,"fix142-fresh-admission"));
  73:     private final Set<String> activeProtection=ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  74:     private final ExecutorService protectionPool=new ThreadPoolExecutor(2,2,0,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS,new SynchronousQueue<>(),r->new Thread(r,"fix142-protection-worker"),new ThreadPoolExecutor.AbortPolicy());
  75:     private int nextProtectionSymbol;

  79:     private final ScheduledExecutorService monitorClock=Executors.newSingleThreadScheduledExecutor(r->new Thread(r,"fix138-analysis-monitor"));
  80:     private final Map<String,Long> discoveryLogTimes=new HashMap<>();
  81:     private volatile String lastSourceStatus="";
  82:     private volatile long lastSourceLog;
  83:     private void sourceStatus(String status,String detail) {
  84:         if(status.equals(lastSourceStatus))return;
  85:         jdbc.update("INSERT INTO shared_market_health(component,status,detail) VALUES('SOURCE',?,?) ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE status=VALUES(status),detail=VALUES(detail)",status,detail);
  86:         lastSourceStatus=status;
  87:     }
  88:     public SharedMarketConsumer(SharedMarketSource source,JdbcTemplate jdbc,CoinConfigurationService coins,
  89:             LivePositionProtectionService protection,PriceMoveMonitorService observer,CandleClosedAnalysisWorker analysis,
  90:             @Qualifier("sharedLiveAnalysisExecutor") Executor executor,
  91:             @Qualifier("sharedHistoricalAnalysisExecutor") Executor historicalExecutor,PlatformTransactionManager manager) {
  92:         this.source=source;this.jdbc=jdbc;this.coins=coins;this.protection=protection;this.observer=observer;
  93:         readinessJdbc=new JdbcTemplate(Objects.requireNonNull(jdbc.getDataSource()));
  94:         readinessJdbc.setQueryTimeout(2);
  95:         readinessTransaction=new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  96:         readinessTransaction.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
  97:         readinessTransaction.setTimeout(2);
  98:         automaticActivation=new SharedAutomaticActivation(source,readinessJdbc,manager);
  99:         this.analysis=analysis;protocol=new Fix142DeliveryProtocol(jdbc);transaction=new TransactionTemplate(manager);
 100:         analysisDispatch=new SharedAnalysisDispatcher(source,jdbc,coins,
 101:             ()->intervals==null?List.of("1m"):intervals.getIntervals(),analysis,executor,historicalExecutor,manager);
 102:         analysisDispatch.admissionGate(this::symbolAllowed);
 103:         transaction.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
 104:     }
 105:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.activation-approved:false}")
 106:     private boolean activationApproved;
 107:     // FIX-140: disabling history is explicit; candles and saved decision context remain intact.
 108:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.analysis.historical-enabled:true}")
 109:     private boolean historicalEnabled=true;
 110:     // FIX-141 Phase 2 review correction: reconcileOwnerTimeoutReviews() no longer mutates
 111:     // analysis_status - it only logs candidates for manual review (see
 112:     // SharedAnalysisDispatcher#reconcileOwnerTimeoutReviews). This flag now gates whether
 113:     // that diagnostic logging runs at all; off by default.
 114:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.analysis.owner-timeout-reconciliation-enabled:false}")
 115:     private boolean ownerTimeoutReconciliationEnabled=false;
 116:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.analysis.owner-timeout-reconciliation-quiet-seconds:3600}")
 117:     private long ownerTimeoutReconciliationQuietSeconds=3600;
 118:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired
 119:     private com.crypto.client.config.binance.BinanceMarketDataProperties intervals;
 120: 
 121:     @org.springframework.context.event.EventListener(org.springframework.boot.context.event.ApplicationReadyEvent.class)
 122:     public void start() {
 123:         if(fix142Enabled && !source.enabled())throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-142 live scheduling requires LIVE mode");
 124:         if(admissionCeiling<2 || freshReserve<1 || freshReserve>=admissionCeiling)
 125:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-142 invalid admission ceiling/reserve");
 126:         protocol.ceiling=admissionCeiling;protocol.reserve=freshReserve;
 127:         analysisDispatch.historicalCapacity(historicalThreads);
 128:         analysisDispatch.historicalEnabled(historicalEnabled);
 129:         analysisDispatch.ownerTimeoutReconciliationEnabled(ownerTimeoutReconciliationEnabled);
 130:         analysisDispatch.ownerTimeoutQuietSeconds(ownerTimeoutReconciliationQuietSeconds);
 131:         // FIX-146: do not throw ApplicationReadyEvent for recoverable symbol state.
 132:         // Every dispatch surface uses this gate; diagnostics and operator APIs stay up.
 133:         initializeTradingReadiness();
 134:         analysisDispatch.acknowledgmentEnabled(acknowledgmentEnabled);
 135:         analysisDispatch.freshPriority(fix142Enabled);
 136:         if("OFF".equals(source.mode()))return;
 137:         if(fix142Enabled) {
 138:             freshClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::discoverFresh,0,100,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 139:             protectionClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::dispatchProtectionTicks,0,50,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 140:         }
 141:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(analysisDispatch::reconcileCompletedWorkAcknowledgments,60,60,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 142:         if("OFF".equals(source.mode()))return;
 143:         if(source.enabled())readinessClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::refreshTradingReadiness,10,10,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 144:         // Independent clocks: slow source reads or CallerRuns analysis saturation
 145:         // cannot monopolize Spring's default scheduler and stop price dispatch.
 146:         discoveryClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::discover,0,100,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 147:         priceClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::dispatchPrices,0,50,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 148:         analysisClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::dispatchAnalysis,0,100,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 149:         if(historicalEnabled)historyClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::dispatchHistoricalAnalysis,1000,1000,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 150:         log.info("[FIX-140][HISTORY_CONFIGURATION] enabled={}, savedSignalsAndCandlesRetained=true",historicalEnabled);
 151:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(analysisDispatch::monitor,30,30,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 152:         // FIX-142 review correction: old protection attempts age out of the recent-price
 153:         // selector, so fencing/alerting must not depend on another claim selecting them.
 154:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::monitorProtectionAttempts,5,5,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 155:         // FIX-141 Phase 2: runs independently of claim()'s REVIEW_BATCH_LIMIT check, so a lane
 156:         // that crossed 16 unresolved reviews can still drain over successive runs.
 157:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(analysisDispatch::reconcileAnalysisOnlyReviews,60,60,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 158:         // FIX-141 Phase 2 review correction: owner-timeout reconciliation is diagnostic-only
 159:         // (logs candidates, performs no mutation) and off until the team enables it and sets a
 160:         // quiet window (see SharedAnalysisDispatcher#ownerTimeoutReconciliationEnabled/Seconds);
 161:         // scheduling it unconditionally here is safe since it no-ops while disabled, and even
 162:         // enabled it cannot change analysis_status itself.
 163:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(analysisDispatch::reconcileOwnerTimeoutReviews,300,300,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 164:         log.info("[FIX-138][ANALYSIS_CONFIGURATION] liveThreads=8, historyThreads={}, independentClocks=true, livePriority=true, historicalWalletExecution=false",historicalThreads);
 165:     }
 166:     public void validateCutover() {
 167:         log.info("[FIX-132][SOURCE_CONFIGURATION] mode={}, readPool=3, priceWorkers=2, pendingPerSymbol=200, publicationSource=collector",source.mode());
 168:         if(!source.enabled())return;
 169:         if(!activationApproved)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 LIVE requires explicit measured cutover acceptance (activation-approved)");
 170:         var unattributed=jdbc.queryForList(OperatorWorkCancellation.UNATTRIBUTED_BLOCKERS);
 171:         if(!unattributed.isEmpty())throw new IllegalStateException("[FIX-132][UNATTRIBUTED_WORK] reconcile before LIVE: "+unattributed);
 172:         var invalidOwners=jdbc.queryForList("""
 173:             SELECT w.signal_id,w.status,w.source_event_id FROM signal_processing_work w
 174:             LEFT JOIN shared_market_event_delivery d ON d.source_event_id=w.source_event_id
 175:             WHERE w.source_event_id IS NOT NULL AND w.status NOT IN ('COMPLETED','EXPIRED','LOCK_RETRY_EXHAUSTED')
 176:             AND (d.source_event_id IS NULL OR w.symbol<>d.symbol OR w.interval_code<>d.interval_code
 177:                  OR w.candle_open_time<>d.candle_open_time OR d.closed=0
 178:                  OR d.analysis_status NOT IN ('RUNNING','REVIEW_REQUIRED','EXECUTION_RETRY')) ORDER BY w.signal_id LIMIT 25
 179:             """);
 180:         if(!invalidOwners.isEmpty())throw new IllegalStateException("[FIX-132][PROCESSING_OWNER_INCONSISTENT] reconcile before LIVE: "+invalidOwners);
 181:         for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols())validateSymbolCutover(symbol);
 182:     }
 183:     // Shared by strict acceptance checks and per-symbol operational readiness.
 184:     private Map<String,Object> validateSymbolCutover(String symbol) {
 185:             var state=readinessJdbc.queryForList("SELECT * FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=?",symbol);
 186:             if(state.isEmpty() || !SharedCutoverPolicy.approved(state.getFirst()))
 187:                 throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 missing/unapproved/review-required cutover state for "+symbol);
 188:             var boundary=state.getFirst();
 189:             if(num(boundary,"cutover_sequence")<0 || num(boundary,"discovered_sequence")<num(boundary,"cutover_sequence"))
 190:                 throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 invalid sequence boundary for "+symbol);
 191:             Integer approval=readinessJdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_cutover_approval WHERE symbol=? AND cutover_sequence=? AND cutover_at=? AND approved_by=? AND approved_at=? AND approval_reference=?",Integer.class,
 192:                 symbol,boundary.get("approved_sequence"),boundary.get("approved_cutover_at"),boundary.get("approved_by"),boundary.get("approved_at"),boundary.get("approval_reference"));
 193:             if(approval==0)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 missing approval audit for "+symbol);
 194:             Long latest=source.feedReader().queryForObject("SELECT last_sequence FROM market_data_stream_cursor WHERE symbol=?",Long.class,symbol);
 195:             if(latest==null || latest<num(state.getFirst(),"discovered_sequence"))throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 source sequence regressed for "+symbol);
 196:             Set<String> required=new HashSet<>(intervals.getIntervals());required.add("1m");
 197:             for(String interval:required) {
 198:                 Long count=source.reader().queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM (SELECT id FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 LIMIT 300) c",Long.class,symbol,interval);
 199:                 if(count==null || count<300)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 incomplete source history: "+symbol+" "+interval);
 200:             }
 201:             return boundary;
 202:     }
 203: 
 204:     private boolean symbolAllowed(String symbol) {
 205:         return !source.enabled() || (readinessSymbols.contains(symbol) || !tradingReadiness.managed())
 206:             && tradingReadiness.allowed(symbol);
 207:     }
 208:     public Map<String,Object> tradingReadiness() {
 209:         var snapshot=new LinkedHashMap<String,Object>(tradingReadiness.snapshot());
 210:         snapshot.put("configuredSymbols",readinessSymbols);
 211:         snapshot.put("automaticFirstCutoverEnabled",automaticFirstCutoverEnabled);
 212:         snapshot.put("waitingCutoverDoesNotAutoApprove",!automaticFirstCutoverEnabled);
 213:         return snapshot;
 214:     }
 215:     void initializeTradingReadiness() {
 216:         if(!source.enabled())return;
 217:         tradingReadiness.begin();
 218:         refreshTradingReadiness();
 219:         log.warn("[FIX-146][STARTUP_READINESS] {}; process stays available, validated first cutovers may activate automatically, uncertain symbols receive no new shared attempts",tradingReadiness.snapshot());
 220:     }
 221:     private static final String SYMBOL_LEGACY_BLOCKERS=OperatorWorkCancellation.UNATTRIBUTED_BLOCKERS
 222:         .replace("WHERE w.source_event_id IS NULL","WHERE w.symbol=? AND w.source_event_id IS NULL");
 223:     private static final String UNSCOPED_BLOCKERS=OperatorWorkCancellation.UNATTRIBUTED_BLOCKERS
 224:         .replace("WHERE w.source_event_id IS NULL","WHERE (w.symbol IS NULL OR TRIM(w.symbol)='') AND w.source_event_id IS NULL");
 225:     // Dedicated clock and round-robin budget: one slow probe cannot monopolize trading
 226:     // clocks or permanently strand later symbols. JDBC timeouts are not total deadlines.
 227:     synchronized void refreshTradingReadiness() {
 228:         if(stopped || !source.enabled())return;
 229:         List<String> symbols;
 230:         try {symbols=List.copyOf(coins.enabledSymbols());}
 231:         catch(RuntimeException failure) {
 232:             for(String symbol:readinessSymbols)recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.CHECK_FAILED);
 233:             log.error("[FIX-146][READINESS_INVENTORY_FAILED]",failure);return;
 234:         }
 235:         readinessSymbols=symbols;tradingReadiness.retainSymbols(symbols);
 236:         boolean unscoped;
 237:         try {unscoped=!readinessJdbc.queryForList(UNSCOPED_BLOCKERS).isEmpty()
 238:                 || !readinessJdbc.queryForList("SELECT signal_id FROM signal_processing_work WHERE source_event_id IS NOT NULL AND (symbol IS NULL OR TRIM(symbol)='') AND status NOT IN ('COMPLETED','EXPIRED','LOCK_RETRY_EXHAUSTED') LIMIT 1").isEmpty();}
 239:         catch(RuntimeException failure) {
 240:             for(String symbol:symbols)recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.CHECK_FAILED);
 241:             log.error("[FIX-146][READINESS_SCOPE_CHECK_FAILED]",failure);return;
 242:         }
 243:         long deadline=System.nanoTime()+TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(20);
 244:         for(int checked=0;checked<symbols.size();checked++) {
 245:             if(checked>0 && System.nanoTime()>=deadline)break;
 246:             String symbol=symbols.get(Math.floorMod(readinessCursor++,symbols.size()));
 247:             if(unscoped)recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.UNSCOPED_WORK);
 248:             else if(!activationApproved)recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.ACTIVATION_REQUIRED);
 249:             else {
 250:                 try {
 251:                     if(!readinessJdbc.queryForList(SYMBOL_LEGACY_BLOCKERS,symbol).isEmpty()) {
 252:                         recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.LEGACY_WORK_REVIEW);continue;
 253:                     }
 254:                     // Inconsistent cross-symbol ownership blocks both affected symbols.
 255:                     var owners=readinessJdbc.queryForList("""
 256:                         SELECT w.signal_id FROM signal_processing_work w
 257:                         LEFT JOIN shared_market_event_delivery d ON d.source_event_id=w.source_event_id
 258:                         WHERE (w.symbol=? OR d.symbol=?) AND w.source_event_id IS NOT NULL
 259:                           AND w.status NOT IN ('COMPLETED','EXPIRED','LOCK_RETRY_EXHAUSTED')
 260:                           AND (w.status='OPERATOR_CANCELLED' OR d.source_event_id IS NULL OR w.symbol IS NULL
 261:                             OR w.interval_code IS NULL OR w.candle_open_time IS NULL OR d.symbol IS NULL
 262:                             OR d.interval_code IS NULL OR d.candle_open_time IS NULL OR d.closed IS NULL
 263:                             OR d.analysis_status IS NULL OR w.symbol<>d.symbol
 264:                             OR w.interval_code<>d.interval_code OR w.candle_open_time<>d.candle_open_time
 265:                             OR d.closed=0 OR d.analysis_status NOT IN ('RUNNING','REVIEW_REQUIRED','EXECUTION_RETRY'))
 266:                         LIMIT 1
 267:                         """,symbol,symbol);
 268:                     if(!owners.isEmpty()) {recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.OWNER_INCONSISTENT);continue;}
 269:                     if(automaticFirstCutoverEnabled)automaticActivation.activateFirst(symbol,intervals.getIntervals());
 270:                     var validated=validateSymbolCutover(symbol);
 271:                     if(!fix142Enabled && !readinessJdbc.queryForList("SELECT symbol FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=? AND active_delivery_state<>'NONE'",symbol).isEmpty()) {
 272:                         recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.OWNERSHIP_MODE_MISMATCH);continue;
 273:                     }
 274:                     if(fix142Enabled && !readinessJdbc.queryForList("""
 275:                         SELECT d.source_event_id FROM shared_market_event_delivery d WHERE d.symbol=? AND phase BETWEEN 1 AND 3
 276:                         AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM shared_market_consumer_state s WHERE s.symbol=d.symbol
 277:                           AND s.active_delivery_event_id=d.source_event_id AND s.active_delivery_state IN ('ACTIVE','REVIEW_REQUIRED')) LIMIT 1
 278:                         """,symbol).isEmpty()) {
 279:                         recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.PARTIAL_DELIVERY);continue;
 280:                     }
 281:                     // Avoid periodic INSERT/locking for already published history. A changed
 282:                     // boundary is revalidated next pass; dispatch still checks durable approval.
 283:                     Integer history=readinessJdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_cutover_history WHERE symbol=? AND cutover_at=? AND cutover_sequence=?",Integer.class,
 284:                         symbol,validated.get("cutover_at"),validated.get("cutover_sequence"));
 285:                     Boolean stillApproved=history!=null && history>0;
 286:                     if(!stillApproved)stillApproved=readinessTransaction.execute(tx->{
 287:                         var state=readinessJdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=? FOR UPDATE",symbol);
 288:                         if(!SharedCutoverPolicy.approved(state))return false;
 289:                         for(String field:List.of("cutover_at","cutover_sequence","approved_sequence","approved_cutover_at","approved_at","approved_by","approval_reference"))
 290:                             if(!Objects.equals(state.get(field),validated.get(field)))return false;
 291:                         readinessJdbc.update("INSERT IGNORE INTO shared_market_cutover_history(symbol,cutover_at,cutover_sequence) VALUES(?,?,?)",
 292:                             symbol,state.get("cutover_at"),state.get("cutover_sequence"));return true;
 293:                     });
 294:                     recordReadiness(symbol,Boolean.TRUE.equals(stillApproved)?SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.READY:SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.CUTOVER_REQUIRED);
 295:                 } catch(RuntimeException failure) {
 296:                     recordReadiness(symbol,readinessReason(failure));
 297:                     log.debug("[FIX-146][READINESS_CHECK_FAILED] symbol={}",symbol,failure);
 298:                 }
 299:             }
 300:             if(System.nanoTime()>=deadline)break;
 301:         }
 302:     }
 303:     private SharedTradingReadiness.Reason readinessReason(RuntimeException failure) {
 304:         String message=String.valueOf(failure.getMessage());
 305:         if(message.contains("missing/unapproved/review-required cutover"))return automaticFirstCutoverEnabled
 306:             ?SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.AUTOMATIC_ACTIVATION_WAITING:SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.CUTOVER_REQUIRED;
 307:         if(message.contains("missing approval audit"))return SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.APPROVAL_AUDIT_MISSING;
 308:         if(message.contains("invalid sequence boundary"))return SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.INVALID_BOUNDARY;
 309:         if(message.contains("source sequence regressed"))return SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.SOURCE_REGRESSED;
 310:         if(message.contains("incomplete source history"))return SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.SOURCE_HISTORY_MISSING;
 311:         return SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.CHECK_FAILED;
 312:     }
 313:     private void recordReadiness(String symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason reason) {
 314:         var before=tradingReadiness.record(symbol,reason);
 315:         if(before==null || before.reason()!=reason) {
 316:             Fix142Metrics.count("READINESS_"+reason.name());
 317:             log.warn("[FIX-146][SYMBOL_READINESS] symbol={}, reason={}, checkedUtc={}, checkedKsa={}; audited first-activation policy, no uncertain retry",
 318:                 symbol,reason,Instant.now(),Instant.now().atOffset(java.time.ZoneOffset.ofHours(3)));
 319:         }
 320:     }
 321: 
 322:     public void discover() {
 323:         if(stopped || "OFF".equals(source.mode())) return;
 324:         try {
 325:             for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols()) {
 326:                 if(!symbolAllowed(symbol))continue;
 327:                 var state=jdbc.queryForList("SELECT * FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=?",symbol);
 328:                 if(state.isEmpty()) {
 329:                     if(source.enabled()) { log.error("[FIX-132][REVIEW_REQUIRED] symbol={}; explicit cutover boundary missing",symbol); continue; }
 330:                     var seq=source.feedReader().queryForList("SELECT last_sequence FROM market_data_stream_cursor WHERE symbol=?",symbol);
 331:                     if(seq.isEmpty()) continue;
 332:                     long n=((Number)seq.getFirst().get("last_sequence")).longValue();
