 130:         analysisDispatch.ownerTimeoutQuietSeconds(ownerTimeoutReconciliationQuietSeconds);
 131:         // FIX-146: do not throw ApplicationReadyEvent for recoverable symbol state.
 132:         // Every dispatch surface uses this gate; diagnostics and operator APIs stay up.
 133:         initializeTradingReadiness();
 134:         analysisDispatch.acknowledgmentEnabled(acknowledgmentEnabled);
 135:         analysisDispatch.freshPriority(fix142Enabled);
 136:         if("OFF".equals(source.mode()))return;
 137:         if(fix142Enabled) {
 138:             freshClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::discoverFresh,0,100,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 139:             protectionClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::dispatchProtectionTicks,0,50,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 140:         }
 141:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(analysisDispatch::reconcileCompletedWorkAcknowledgments,60,60,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 142:         if("OFF".equals(source.mode()))return;
 143:         if(source.enabled())readinessClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::refreshTradingReadiness,10,10,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 144:         // Independent clocks: slow source reads or CallerRuns analysis saturation
 145:         // cannot monopolize Spring's default scheduler and stop price dispatch.
 146:         discoveryClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::discover,0,100,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 147:         priceClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::dispatchPrices,0,50,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 148:         analysisClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::dispatchAnalysis,0,100,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 149:         if(historicalEnabled)historyClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::dispatchHistoricalAnalysis,1000,1000,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 150:         log.info("[FIX-140][HISTORY_CONFIGURATION] enabled={}, savedSignalsAndCandlesRetained=true",historicalEnabled);
 151:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(analysisDispatch::monitor,30,30,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 152:         // FIX-142 review correction: old protection attempts age out of the recent-price
 153:         // selector, so fencing/alerting must not depend on another claim selecting them.
 154:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::monitorProtectionAttempts,5,5,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 155:         // FIX-141 Phase 2: runs independently of claim()'s REVIEW_BATCH_LIMIT check, so a lane
 156:         // that crossed 16 unresolved reviews can still drain over successive runs.
 157:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(analysisDispatch::reconcileAnalysisOnlyReviews,60,60,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 158:         // FIX-141 Phase 2 review correction: owner-timeout reconciliation is diagnostic-only
 159:         // (logs candidates, performs no mutation) and off until the team enables it and sets a
 160:         // quiet window (see SharedAnalysisDispatcher#ownerTimeoutReconciliationEnabled/Seconds);
 161:         // scheduling it unconditionally here is safe since it no-ops while disabled, and even
 162:         // enabled it cannot change analysis_status itself.
 163:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(analysisDispatch::reconcileOwnerTimeoutReviews,300,300,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 164:         log.info("[FIX-138][ANALYSIS_CONFIGURATION] liveThreads=8, historyThreads={}, independentClocks=true, livePriority=true, historicalWalletExecution=false",historicalThreads);
 165:     }
 166:     public void validateCutover() {
 167:         log.info("[FIX-132][SOURCE_CONFIGURATION] mode={}, readPool=3, priceWorkers=2, pendingPerSymbol=200, publicationSource=collector",source.mode());
 168:         if(!source.enabled())return;
 169:         if(!activationApproved)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 LIVE requires explicit measured cutover acceptance (activation-approved)");
 170:         var unattributed=jdbc.queryForList(OperatorWorkCancellation.UNATTRIBUTED_BLOCKERS);

### src/main/java/com/crypto/service/MarketDataService.java
   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import java.util.Comparator;
   4: import java.util.List;
   5: import java.util.Locale;
   6: 
   7: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   8: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
   9: import org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager;
  10: import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate;
  11: 
  12: import com.crypto.client.binance.BinanceMarketDataClient;
  13: import com.crypto.client.binance.dto.BinanceKline;
  14: import com.crypto.domain.Candle;
  15: import com.crypto.repository.CandleRepository;
  16: 
  17: @Service
  18: public class MarketDataService {
  19:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("#{'${shared-market.mode:OFF}' == 'LIVE'}")
  20:     private boolean sharedMarketEnabled;
  21: 
  22: 
  23:     private final BinanceMarketDataClient client;
  24:     private final CandleRepository candleRepository;
  25:     private final TransactionTemplate transaction;
  26: 
  27:     public MarketDataService(BinanceMarketDataClient client,CandleRepository candleRepository,PlatformTransactionManager manager){
  28:         this.client=client;this.candleRepository=candleRepository;this.transaction=new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  29:     }
  30: 
  31:     public int importCandles(String symbol, String interval, int limit) {
  32:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132: collector owns candle imports"); }
  33:         String normalizedSymbol = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  34:         // Do not trust a potentially skewed local clock to finalise Binance's
  35:         // currently-open REST candle. Read it before klines: a later timestamp
  36:         // could otherwise turn an in-flight response row into a false close.
  37:         java.time.Instant exchangeNow=client.getServerTime();
  38:         List<BinanceKline> klines = client.getKlines(normalizedSymbol, interval, limit);
  39:         // No database transaction is open while Binance is contacted. The local
  40:         // writes remain one transaction once the bounded network calls return.
  41:         Integer upserted=transaction.execute(status->{
  42:             int count=0;
  43:             for (BinanceKline kline : klines) {
  44:                 // FIX-153: an in-progress REST candle is not a close event or historical evidence.
  45:                 if (kline.closeTime().isAfter(exchangeNow)) continue;
  46:                 count += candleRepository.upsert(
  47:                         normalizedSymbol,interval,kline.openTime(),kline.closeTime(),
  48:                         kline.openPrice(),kline.highPrice(),kline.lowPrice(),kline.closePrice(),
  49:                         kline.volume(),kline.quoteAssetVolume(),kline.numberOfTrades(),
  50:                         kline.takerBuyBaseVolume(),kline.takerBuyQuoteVolume(),true);
  51:             }
  52:             return count;
  53:         });
  54:         if(upserted==null)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 local candle import transaction returned no result");
  55:         return upserted;
  56:     }
  57: 
  58:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
  59:     public List<Candle> latestCandles(String symbol, String interval) {
  60:         return candleRepository
  61:                 .findTop500BySymbolAndIntervalCodeOrderByOpenTimeDesc(
  62:                         symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT), interval)
  63:                 .stream()
  64:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(Candle::getOpenTime))
  65:                 .toList();
  66:     }
  67: 
  68:     private Candle toEntity(String symbol, String interval, BinanceKline kline) {
  69:         return Candle.builder()
  70:                 .symbol(symbol)
  71:                 .intervalCode(interval)
  72:                 .openTime(kline.openTime())
  73:                 .closeTime(kline.closeTime())
  74:                 .openPrice(kline.openPrice())
  75:                 .highPrice(kline.highPrice())
  76:                 .lowPrice(kline.lowPrice())
  77:                 .closePrice(kline.closePrice())
  78:                 .volume(kline.volume())
  79:                 .quoteAssetVolume(kline.quoteAssetVolume())
  80:                 .numberOfTrades(kline.numberOfTrades())
  81:                 .takerBuyBaseVolume(kline.takerBuyBaseVolume())
  82:                 .takerBuyQuoteVolume(kline.takerBuyQuoteVolume())
  83:                 .closed(true)
  84:                 .build();
  85:     }
  86: }

### src/main/java/com/crypto/client/binance/websocket/BinanceWebSocketManager.java
   1: package com.crypto.client.binance.websocket;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.client.config.binance.BinanceMarketDataProperties;
   4: import com.crypto.service.BinanceKlineService;
   5: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
   6: import jakarta.annotation.PreDestroy;
   7: import java.net.URI;
   8: import java.time.Instant;
   9: import java.util.ArrayList;
  10: import java.util.List;
  11: import java.util.concurrent.*;
  12: import java.util.concurrent.locks.LockSupport;
  13: import java.util.function.Supplier;
  14: import org.slf4j.Logger;
  15: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
  16: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
  17: import org.springframework.boot.context.event.ApplicationReadyEvent;
  18: import org.springframework.context.event.EventListener;
  19: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
  20: import org.springframework.web.socket.WebSocketSession;
  21: import org.springframework.web.socket.client.standard.StandardWebSocketClient;
  22: 
  23: /** FIX-131: one owner for startup/reload/health. The actor never does database or socket I/O.
  24:  * A timed-out, unresolved handshake is retained: cancellation alone cannot prove no late socket.
  25:  * Availability is deliberately sacrificed until old work AND transport have terminated. */
  26: @Component
  27: public class BinanceWebSocketManager {
  28:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("#{'${shared-market.mode:OFF}' == 'LIVE'}")
  29:     private boolean sharedMarketEnabled;
  30: 
  31:     private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(BinanceWebSocketManager.class);
  32:     static final long SCAN_NANOS = TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS.toNanos(500);
  33:     static final long DEADLINE_NANOS = TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(15);
  34:     interface Connector { CompletableFuture<WebSocketSession> connect(BinanceWebSocketHandler handler, String url); }
  35:     private final BinanceMarketDataProperties properties;
  36:     private final Supplier<String> urls;
  37:     private final ObjectMapper mapper;
  38:     private final BinanceKlineService service;
  39:     private final Connector connector;
  40:     private final CandleGapDiagnostics gaps;
  41:     @Autowired(required=false) private com.crypto.local.LocalCandleReconciliationService reconciliation;
  42:     private final java.util.function.LongSupplier nanos;
  43:     private final ArrayBlockingQueue<CompletableFuture<Void>> requests = new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(32);
  44:     private final ThreadPoolExecutor connectWorker = worker("fix131-connect", 1);
  45:     private final ThreadPoolExecutor closeWorker = worker("fix131-close", 1);
  46:     private final Object lifecycle = new Object();
  47:     private final CompletableFuture<Void> stopped = new CompletableFuture<>();
  48:     private final List<CompletableFuture<Void>> waiting = new ArrayList<>();
  49:     private final java.util.Set<BinanceWebSocketHandler> cleanup = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  50:     private final java.util.Set<BinanceWebSocketHandler> cleaning = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  51:     private volatile boolean stopRequested;
  52:     private volatile Thread actor;
  53:     private volatile String phase = "STOPPED";
  54:     private volatile Attempt current;
  55:     private long sequence;
  56:     private long interruptionStart;
  57:     private Instant revokedAt;
  58:     private long nextAttempt;
  59:     private boolean replacement;
  60:     private static final class Attempt {
  61:         final BinanceWebSocketHandler handler;
  62:         final long started;
  63:         volatile boolean settled;
  64:         volatile Throwable failure;
  65:         volatile String url;
  66:         volatile boolean closing;
  67:         long nextClose;
  68:         long revoked;
  69:         boolean timeoutLogged;
  70:         long nextSilenceLog;
  71:         Attempt(BinanceWebSocketHandler handler, long started) { this.handler = handler; this.started = started; }
  72:     }
  73:     @Autowired
  74:     public BinanceWebSocketManager(BinanceMarketDataProperties properties, BinanceStreamUrlBuilder builder,
  75:             ObjectMapper mapper, BinanceKlineService service, CandleGapDiagnostics gaps) {
  76:         this(properties, builder::build, mapper, service,
  77:                 (handler, url) -> new StandardWebSocketClient().execute(handler, null, URI.create(url)), gaps);
  78:     }
  79:     BinanceWebSocketManager(BinanceMarketDataProperties properties, Supplier<String> urls,
  80:             ObjectMapper mapper, BinanceKlineService service, Connector connector, CandleGapDiagnostics gaps) {
  81:         this(properties, urls, mapper, service, connector, gaps, System::nanoTime);
  82:     }
  83:     BinanceWebSocketManager(BinanceMarketDataProperties properties, Supplier<String> urls,
  84:             ObjectMapper mapper, BinanceKlineService service, Connector connector, CandleGapDiagnostics gaps,
  85:             java.util.function.LongSupplier nanos) {
  86:         this.nanos = nanos;
  87:         this.properties = properties; this.urls = urls; this.mapper = mapper; this.service = service;
  88:         this.connector = connector; this.gaps = gaps;
  89:     }
  90:     private static ThreadPoolExecutor worker(String name, int queue) {
  91:         return new ThreadPoolExecutor(1, 1, 0, TimeUnit.SECONDS, new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(queue), task -> {
  92:             Thread t = new Thread(task, name); t.setDaemon(true); return t;
  93:         }, new ThreadPoolExecutor.AbortPolicy());
  94:     }
  95:     @EventListener(ApplicationReadyEvent.class)
  96:     public void start() {
  97:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
  98:         synchronized (lifecycle) {
  99:             if (actor != null || stopRequested || !properties.getWebsocket().isEnabled()) return;
 100:             replacement = true;
 101:             actor = new Thread(this::run, "fix131-ws-owner");
 102:             actor.setDaemon(true);
 103:             actor.start();
 104:         }
 105:     }
 106:     void wake() { LockSupport.unpark(actor); }
 107:     private void notice(BinanceWebSocketHandler handler) {
 108:         if (handler.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.REVOKED && !handler.allClosed()) {
 109:             if (current == null || current.handler != handler) cleanup.add(handler);
 110:             // After bounded shutdown, even the unresolved CURRENT handshake retains a cleanup path.
 111:             // This fallback runs only during shutdown, never on a live processing callback.
 112:             if (closeWorker.isShutdown() && handler.gate.isDrained()) handler.close();
 113:         }
 114:         wake();
 115:     }
 116:     CompletableFuture<Void> requestReload() {
 117:         if (!properties.getWebsocket().isEnabled()) throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.CONNECTION_FAILED);
 118:         var request = new CompletableFuture<Void>();
 119:         synchronized (lifecycle) {
 120:             if (stopRequested) throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.STOPPED);
 121:             if (!requests.offer(request)) throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.BUSY);
 122:         }
 123:         start(); wake();
 124:         return request;
 125:     }
 126:     public void reload() {
 127:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
 128:         var request = requestReload();
 129:         try { request.get(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS); }
 130:         catch (TimeoutException ex) { throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.WAIT_TIMEOUT); }
 131:         catch (InterruptedException ex) { Thread.currentThread().interrupt(); throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.WAIT_TIMEOUT); }
 132:         catch (ExecutionException ex) {
 133:             if (ex.getCause() instanceof RuntimeException runtime) throw runtime;
 134:             throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.CONNECTION_FAILED);
 135:         }
 136:     }
 137:     private WebSocketReconnectException failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason reason) {
 138:         return new WebSocketReconnectException(reason, phase);
 139:     }
 140:     private void run() {
 141:         try {
 142:             while (true) {
 143:                 // Persistent lifecycle facts are examined on EVERY pass, before more queued requests.
 144:                 tick();
 145:                 if (stopRequested && current == null && cleanup.isEmpty()) break;
 146:                 LockSupport.parkNanos(SCAN_NANOS);
 147:             }
 148:             phase = "STOPPED";
 149:             finish(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.STOPPED);
 150:             stopped.complete(null);
 151:         } catch (Throwable ex) {
 152:             log.error("[FIX-131][WS-LIFECYCLE][OWNER_FAILED] phase={}; restart required", phase, ex);
 153:             synchronized (lifecycle) { stopRequested = true; if (current != null) current.handler.gate.revoke(); }
 154:             finish(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.STOPPED);
 155:             stopped.completeExceptionally(ex);
 156:         }
 157:     }
 158:     private void tick() {
 159:         long now = nanos.getAsLong();
 160:         for (var h : cleanup) {
 161:             if (h.allClosed()) { cleanup.remove(h); continue; }
 162:             if (h.gate.isDrained() && cleaning.add(h)) {
 163:                 try { closeWorker.execute(() -> {
 164:                     try { h.close(); } finally { cleaning.remove(h); wake(); }
 165:                 }); } catch (RejectedExecutionException ex) { cleaning.remove(h); }
 166:             }
 167:         }
 168:         // Bound callers retained off-mailbox as well as mailbox capacity.
 169:         CompletableFuture<Void> request;
 170:         for (int n = 0; n < 32 && (request = requests.poll()) != null; n++) {

### src/main/java/com/crypto/client/binance/websocket/BinanceStreamUrlBuilder.java
   1: package com.crypto.client.binance.websocket;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.administration.service.CoinConfigurationService;
   4: import com.crypto.client.config.binance.BinanceMarketDataProperties;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   6: 
   7: import java.util.List;
   8: import java.util.Locale;
   9: import java.util.stream.Collectors;
  10: 
  11: @Component
  12: public class BinanceStreamUrlBuilder {
  13: 
  14:     private final BinanceMarketDataProperties properties;
  15:     private final CoinConfigurationService coinConfigurationService;
  16: 
  17:     public BinanceStreamUrlBuilder(
  18:             BinanceMarketDataProperties properties,
  19:             CoinConfigurationService coinConfigurationService
  20:     ) {
  21:         this.properties = properties;
  22:         this.coinConfigurationService = coinConfigurationService;
  23:     }
  24: 
  25:     public String build() {
  26:         List<String> symbols = coinConfigurationService.enabledSymbols();
  27:         if (symbols.isEmpty()) {
  28:             throw new IllegalStateException("No enabled Binance symbols configured in Administration.");
  29:         }
  30:         if (properties.getIntervals().isEmpty()) {
  31:             throw new IllegalStateException("No Binance intervals configured.");
  32:         }
  33: 
  34:         String streams = symbols.stream()
  35:                 .flatMap(symbol -> properties.getIntervals().stream()
  36:                         .map(interval -> symbol.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)
  37:                                 + "@kline_" + interval.trim()))
  38:                 .collect(Collectors.joining("/"));
  39: 
  40:         return properties.getWebsocketBaseUrl() + "/stream?streams=" + streams;
  41:     }
  42: }

  32:         }

  42:         }
  43:     }
  44: }

  37: 
  38: @Service
  39: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  40: @Slf4j
  41: public class RegressionTestWorker {
  42:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("#{'${shared-market.mode:OFF}' == 'LIVE'}")
  43:     private boolean sharedMarketEnabled;
  44: 
  45: 
  46:     /**
  47:      * Replay warm-up is context-only. It gives the production analysis/execution services
  48:      * the same already-closed 1m/5m/1h history they would have had at the requested start
  49:      * without allowing wallet executions before the requested test window.
  50:      */
  51:     private static final Duration REPLAY_CONTEXT_WARMUP = Duration.ofHours(3);
  52: 
  53:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;
  54:     // FIX-136: field injection preserves the existing constructor used by Replay tests.
  55:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired
  56:     private org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager auditTransactionManager;
  57:     private final CandleRepository candleRepository;
  58:     private final TradeSignalRepository signalRepository;
  59:     private final TechnicalIndicatorService technicalIndicatorService;
  60:     private final AnalysisService analysisService;
  61:     private final ShadowProductionReplayService shadowReplayService;
  62:     private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  63:     private final BtcContextProperties btcContextProperties;
  64:     private final MarketPriceEventService marketPriceEventService;
  65: 
  66:     // FIX-087: heartbeat age alone cannot prove that a long replay worker has died.
  67:     // Some deterministic replay stages can legitimately run for more than the stale-heartbeat
  68:     // threshold without returning to updateRun(). Keep atomic in-JVM ownership so Resume cannot
  69:     // launch a second worker for the same durable run while the first worker is still executing.
  70:     private final Set<Long> activeRunIds = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  71: 
  72:     // FIX-090: cooperative stop requests are tracked separately from active ownership. A Stop Test
  73:     // request never pretends the worker is gone; Delete Data stays blocked until runAsync reaches
  74:     // finally and removes the run from activeRunIds.
  75:     private final Set<Long> stopRequestedRunIds = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  76: 
  77:     public boolean isActive(long runId) {
  78:         return activeRunIds.contains(runId);
  79:     }
  80: 
  81:     public boolean hasActiveRuns() {
  82:         return !activeRunIds.isEmpty();
  83:     }
  84: 
  85:     public boolean requestStop(long runId) {
  86:         if (!activeRunIds.contains(runId)) return false;
  87:         stopRequestedRunIds.add(runId);
  88:         return true;
  89:     }
  90: 
  91:     public boolean isStopRequested(long runId) {
  92:         return stopRequestedRunIds.contains(runId);
  93:     }
  94: 
  95:     private void checkStopRequested(long runId) {

AGENTS.md:109:collector. This is a design decision, not an implemented/deployed change.
AGENTS.md-110-
AGENTS.md-111-- Target runtime: trader-owned Binance kline ingestion and local
AGENTS.md:112:  `crypto_ai.candle` writes with `shared-market.mode=OFF`.
AGENTS.md:113:- The collector remains independent in `crypto_ai_v2`; the trader must not
AGENTS.md:114:  connect to, read from, or write collector stream/cursor tables at runtime.
AGENTS.md:115:- Do not run direct ingestion and the shared-market executors together.
AGENTS.md:116:- Applied shared-market tables and Flyway history are historical evidence; do
AGENTS.md-117-  not drop, reset, or edit them merely to enable the new path.
AGENTS.md:118:- `V103__fix_149_fresh_admission_indexes` still requires
AGENTS.md-119-  `crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event` for a fresh Flyway install. Treat
AGENTS.md-120-  trader-only bootstrap as a release blocker until a forward, tested installer
AGENTS.md-121-  policy exists. Never edit an applied Flyway migration.
AGENTS.md-122-
AGENTS.md:123:## Local-candle implementation safety
AGENTS.md-124-
AGENTS.md:125:When implementing or reviewing the local-candle design:
AGENTS.md-126-
AGENTS.md:127:- Keep `shared-market.fix142.enabled=false`,
AGENTS.md:128:  `shared-market.fix142.acknowledgment-enabled=false`, and scheduled analysis
AGENTS.md:129:  disabled for the OFF-mode cutover unless a reviewed change explicitly says
AGENTS.md-130-  otherwise.
AGENTS.md:131:- Preserve the input -> protection -> observer -> close-dispatch transaction
AGENTS.md-132-  order. Candle close analysis must not run on the Binance ingestion thread.
AGENTS.md-133-- A closed candle needs durable/recoverable analysis admission; an in-memory
--
AGENTS.md-137-  overwrite a closed candle. Publish close work only for an atomic
AGENTS.md-138-  open-to-closed transition.
AGENTS.md:139:- A WebSocket reconnect can occur at a candle-close boundary and Binance may
AGENTS.md:140:  not replay that final closed-kline event. Candle recovery is automatic: use
AGENTS.md-141-  an exact authoritative Binance REST candle only after exchange-time
AGENTS.md-142-  finality and the measured live-close grace. It may either finalise a matching
AGENTS.md-143-  existing `closed=0` row or insert a wholly absent identity inside the active
AGENTS.md-144-  data-quality window. A recovered open row preserves provenance; an inserted
AGENTS.md:145:  gap row uses `BACKFILL` provenance. Neither path may alter a completed row,
AGENTS.md-146-  create a close-transition token, dispatch analysis, create a live signal,
AGENTS.md-147-  or execute a trade. The recovery must have durable retry/audit evidence,
AGENTS.md-148-  bounded rate-limit handling, health visibility, and no manual repair step.
AGENTS.md:149:- Automation direction: no pending candle or analysis outcome may require
AGENTS.md-150-  manual approval, manual database repair, or a persisted trading fence.
AGENTS.md:151:  Reconciliation must retry and resolve automatically, or age out to a durable
AGENTS.md-152-  explicit outcome with a visible reason. This does not permit unverified data
AGENTS.md-153-  to masquerade as valid or force an entry: a genuine strategy/risk veto or
AGENTS.md-154-  unavailable authoritative data remains non-executable evidence, not a
AGENTS.md:155:  silently stuck pending signal.
AGENTS.md:156:- Gap backfill must be a pure insert-if-absent operation. It must never use a
AGENTS.md-157-  duplicate-key update that could finalise or alter a live row which appeared
AGENTS.md-158-  while the authoritative REST request was in progress. Recovery candidates
--
AGENTS.md-170-  durable effect evidence (`EFFECT_COMMITTED` or `EFFECT_ABSENT`); unknown
AGENTS.md-171-  atomicity remains a release blocker. Binance REST rate-limit/ban handling
AGENTS.md:172:  must be shared by reconciliation, clock sync, bootstrap, and other REST
AGENTS.md-173-  callers.
AGENTS.md-174-- Enforce freshness from persisted candle close time before analysis and again
--
AGENTS.md-183-  that direction. In-process coordination and normal database constraints are
AGENTS.md-184-  distinct from persisted trading fences.
AGENTS.md:185:- OFF-mode health/admin endpoints must not query or write retired shared-market
AGENTS.md:186:  tables. Existing `REVIEW_REQUIRED` history must not block OFF-mode work.
AGENTS.md:187:- Keep production and Replay evidence aligned. Replay must fail a parity claim
AGENTS.md-188-  for unresolved direct price-event evidence, while deliberate exclusions stay
AGENTS.md-189-  excluded.
--
AGENTS.md-191-## Delivery discipline
AGENTS.md-192-
AGENTS.md:193:- Treat the local-candle architecture as not deployment-ready until its
AGENTS.md-194-  migration/bootstrap policy, concurrency, crash/restart, stale-input,
AGENTS.md:195:  Replay-parity, and Java 21/MySQL validation gates pass.
AGENTS.md-196-- Do not deploy or change production configuration/data unless the user
AGENTS.md-197-  explicitly requests it.
AGENTS.md-198-- Protection coverage for open positions during application replacement remains
AGENTS.md:199:  an unresolved release gate. Do not represent the local-candle design as
AGENTS.md-200-  deployment-ready until it is implemented or conclusively disproven.
AGENTS.md-201-
AGENTS.md:202:## FIX-154: pending higher-timeframe BUY validation
AGENTS.md-203-
AGENTS.md-204-FIX-154 is a separate, not-yet-implemented design. Do not merge it into the
AGENTS.md:205:local-candle change or describe it as deployed.
AGENTS.md-206-
AGENTS.md-207-Purpose: a historical bearish 1h signal may block or delay a current 1m BUY
--
AGENTS.md-218-- Persist `trade_signal.data_quality_valid` from
AGENTS.md-219-  `MarketContextSnapshot.dataValid()` for every new signal. Historical NULL
AGENTS.md:220:  means unknown/invalid; never backfill it to true.
AGENTS.md-221-- A confirmed 1h recovery may release only a soft conflict and only with fresh,
AGENTS.md-222-  quality-valid, non-bearish 5m evidence.
AGENTS.md-223-- Preserve the legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` behavior: the new data-quality gate
AGENTS.md-224-  applies to the new hard-veto/recovery paths, not that legacy fallback.
AGENTS.md:225:- Add immutable BUY-evaluation audit evidence; maintain Replay parity and
AGENTS.md-226-  prevent future-candle or wall-clock look-ahead.

claude-docs\ARCHITECTURE.md:101:## WebSocket lifecycle
claude-docs\ARCHITECTURE.md-102-
claude-docs\ARCHITECTURE.md:103:`BinanceWebSocketManager` ticks every 500 ms on its owner thread; connect and
claude-docs\ARCHITECTURE.md-104-close run on separate workers. Each attempt gets a new
claude-docs\ARCHITECTURE.md:105:`BinanceWebSocketHandler` + `GenerationGate` (PENDING -> ADMITTING -> REVOKED);
claude-docs\ARCHITECTURE.md-106-messages outside ADMITTING / the primary session are dropped. Handshake
claude-docs\ARCHITECTURE.md:107:deadline 15s. Failure backoff = `websocket.health-check-seconds` (15s);
claude-docs\ARCHITECTURE.md:108:`websocket.reconnect-delay-seconds` is only displayed by
claude-docs\ARCHITECTURE.md-109-`BinanceStatusController` and does not drive reconnects. 30s silence only logs
claude-docs\ARCHITECTURE.md-110-`NO_MESSAGES`. Coin changes call `reload()` (bounded mailbox, 30s wait).

claude-docs\ARCHITECTURE.md-117-  4 (`LANE_OVERFLOW`). Coordinates live and detector admissions.

 464:     /**
 465:      * FIX-11H: loads one immutable replay dataset. The prefix comes from the exact existing
 466:      * closed/as-of query, so missing candle gaps cannot shorten history. The forward segment
 467:      * explicitly requires closed=true. No Production caller reaches this method.
 468:      */
 469:     private ReplayDataset loadDataset(String symbol, String btcSymbol, Instant start, Instant end) {
 470:         Map<ReplayDataset.CandleKey, List<Candle>> candleMap = new HashMap<>();
 471:         Set<String> symbols = new LinkedHashSet<>(List.of(symbol, btcSymbol));
 472:         int historyLimit = technicalIndicatorService.regressionHistoryLimit();
 473:         for (String sym : symbols) {
 474:             for (String interval : List.of("1m", "5m", "1h")) {
 475:                 List<Candle> prefix = new java.util.ArrayList<>(candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
 476:                         sym, interval, start, PageRequest.of(0, historyLimit)));
 477:                 java.util.Collections.reverse(prefix);
 478:                 List<Candle> forward = candleRepository
 479:                         .findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(
 480:                                 sym, interval, start, end);
 481:                 List<Candle> merged = new java.util.ArrayList<>(prefix.size() + forward.size());
 482:                 merged.addAll(prefix);
 483:                 Instant prefixEnd = prefix.isEmpty() ? Instant.EPOCH : prefix.get(prefix.size() - 1).getOpenTime();
 484:                 forward.stream().filter(c -> c.getOpenTime().isAfter(prefixEnd)).forEach(merged::add);
 485:                 candleMap.put(new ReplayDataset.CandleKey(sym, interval), merged);
 486:                 log.info("FIX-11H dataset candles: symbol={}, interval={}, prefix={}, forward={}, merged={}",
 487:                         sym, interval, prefix.size(), forward.size(), merged.size());
 488:             }
 489:         }

  15:  * Trader Flyway history remains in crypto_ai on this same MySQL server.
  16:  * Java migration avoids temporary stored procedures and their extra privileges.

  32:     @Override public void migrate(Context context) throws Exception {

  34:         if (!"MySQL".equals(connection.getMetaData().getDatabaseProductName())

  50:                 }

  59:         }

  61:             LOG.log(System.Logger.Level.INFO, "[FIX-149][INDEXES_ALREADY_PRESENT] schema=crypto_ai_v2 historySchema=crypto_ai utc=" + Instant.now());
  62:             return;
  63:         }

  66:             if (!result.next()) throw new SQLException("FIX-149 cannot read metadata-lock timeout");
  67:             previousLockWait = result.getLong(1);
  68:         }

  70:         long startNanos = System.nanoTime();

## FINDINGS

1. **Implemented default is trader-owned local ingestion.** Checked-in `shared-market.mode` defaults to `OFF`. In OFF, the trader opens Binance combined kline WebSockets, performs REST imports, and writes the local `crypto_ai.candle` table.

2. **LIVE is the only mode that disables direct ingestion.** WebSocket, REST import, and bootstrap check `mode == LIVE`, not `mode != OFF`. Therefore **OBSERVE runs direct Binance ingestion while also connecting to the collector and starting shared-market clocks**. This contradicts the pending direction’s “do not run direct ingestion and shared-market executors together.”

3. **Current local candle close behavior is mostly monotonic.** Runtime kline upsert preserves a completed candle and emits a close-transition token only for an atomic open-to-closed transition. The close event is published after the coordinator’s input, protection, and observer phases.

4. **Ordinary REST import is not compliant recovery/backfill.** It calls the generic BACKFILL upsert. On a duplicate open runtime candle, that SQL updates OHLCV and sets `closed=1`, but preserves provenance and transition token. It produces neither reconciliation audit evidence nor an analysis-outcome record. Its nearby comment claiming “inserts missing identities only” is false.

5. **A real live-close loss interleaving exists.** A REST import can finalize an already-open runtime candle before the final WebSocket close arrives. The later WebSocket upsert sees a completed row and cannot install its token; no normal close event is dispatched. The detector requires a token and reconciliation selects only `closed=0`, so neither path recovers that specific admission gap. Runtime triggers include authenticated `/api/market-data/import` and coin activation, which reloads streams then bootstraps.

6. **The specialised reconciliation implementation is closer to the pending design but is disabled by default.** It waits for exchange-time finality plus configured grace, finalizes only an existing open row, uses insert-only for an absent identity, records audit/outcome evidence, and never dispatches recovery work. However, checked-in `local-candle.reconciliation.enabled=false`.

7. **Enabled reconciliation has unresolved gaps.** Its open-row query applies `LIMIT 50` before due/loss-evidence filtering and does not restrict candidates to enabled symbols. Reconnect evidence is process-local. REST requests have no demonstrated shared handling of Binance 429/418, `Retry-After`, bans, or cross-client request budget.

8. **Close-dispatch durability is bounded, not fully durable.** The detector can repair tokened close-to-dispatch gaps only within a bounded lookback and while the lane is idle. Retry state is in memory. A prolonged outage or sustained busy lane can age a tokened candle outside selection without a terminal durable outcome.

9. **Dispatcher saturation can discard closed-candle analysis admission.** For 1m, a pending candle may be replaced; for higher intervals, a fifth pending item is rejected. These paths log and increment in-memory diagnostics only. The detector may later recover a dropped item, but cannot guarantee it if the lane remains non-idle or the candle passes the lookback window.

10. **Historical-only recovery is behaviorally, not structurally, separated from execution.** The worker branches to `analyzeRecovered` for stale/non-live work and returns before paper trading. But that same class injects `PaperTradingService`; this does not meet AGENTS.md’s stronger structural-isolation requirement.

11. **Replay does not establish local-ingestion parity.** Its ordinary replay loader selects candles by symbol/interval/time without filtering `closed`; the dataset path does filter forward candles by `closed=true`. Neither path models provenance, close tokens, reconciliation audit/outcomes, grace timing, or recovery admission. Replay parity for the pending recovery design is not shown.

12. **Production behavior is unknown.** No production systems, configuration overrides, database history, logs, or runtime processes were accessed.

## OBJECTIONS

- The checked-in OBSERVE mode violates the pending no-dual-ingestion rule.
- Default OFF-mode REST import can consume an active live close without durable analysis/outcome evidence.
- Reconciliation is disabled and, when enabled, lacks demonstrated durable restart evidence, fair candidate selection, and shared rate-limit/ban handling.
- Dispatcher/detector bounds do not prove the required durable terminal outcome for every close path.
- Replay does not prove equivalence of ingestion, recovery, and admission behavior.
- The architecture remains unproven for the pending migration/bootstrap and crash/restart release gates.

## UNKNOWNS

- Whether the identified live-close race has occurred in any environment.
- Whether Binance REST can return an incomplete value at the exact server-time boundary.
- Actual deployed mode, overrides, Flyway history, collector availability, and replacement-time position protection.
- Whether the disposable-MySQL integration test has been run successfully.

Corrections to prior reports:

- **There is not a duplicate top-level `local-candle` YAML key** in the checked-in file. It appears once at line 40. The later top-level key is `shared-market`.
- **The import endpoint is not unauthenticated**: it falls under `.anyRequest().authenticated()`.
- **The detector can write `ANALYSIS_EXPIRED`**, but only after it has observed/retried an eligible candle. It does not solve candles that age out while never eligible for detector admission.
- **The prior claim that Replay universally selects only closed candles was too broad**; its standard `candles()` loader does not filter `closed`.

Documentation contradiction:

- AGENTS.md says V103 remains a trader-only fresh-install blocker, but `B105__fix_153_trader_only_baseline.sql` explicitly says it has no trader-only baseline effect, and `LocalCandleMySqlIT` contains a fresh-baseline test. This is evidence of intended resolution, not proof that the integration test has passed.
- `claude-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` groups LIVE and OBSERVE as shared-only despite implementation disabling direct ingestion only in LIVE.

## VERDICT

REJECTED
